// ---- Renderer / scene ---- var GFX = { aa: true, soft: true, fog: 1 }; // graphics settings, kept in the browser try { var gs = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pr_gfx') || '{}'); if (gs.aa === false) GFX.aa = false; if (gs.soft === false) GFX.soft = false; if (gs.fog >= 0.3 && gs.fog <= 1) GFX.fog = gs.fog; } catch (e) {} var renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas: canvas, antialias: GFX.aa }); renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2)); renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true; renderer.shadowMap.type = GFX.soft ? THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap : THREE.PCFShadowMap; var scene = new THREE.Scene(); scene.background = new THREE.Color(SKY); scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(SKY, 160 * GFX.fog, 640 * GFX.fog); var camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(65, 1, 0.1, 700 * GFX.fog); // See-through: any opaque surface between the camera and Molly is drawn see-through (a fine stipple, most of it left out) inside a narrow cone along the line from the camera to her, so she is never hidden. It stops short of her, so she and the ground at her feet are drawn as usual. var SEE = { on: { value: 0 }, a: { value: new THREE.Vector3() }, b: { value: new THREE.Vector3() }, r: { value: 2.2 }, t1: { value: 0.8 } }; THREE.Material.prototype.onBeforeCompile = function (shader) { if (this.transparent || this.isShaderMaterial || this.isPointsMaterial || this.isLineBasicMaterial || this.isSpriteMaterial || this.userData.noSee) return; if (shader.vertexShader.indexOf('#include ') < 0 || shader.fragmentShader.indexOf('void main() {') < 0) return; shader.uniforms.uSeeOn = SEE.on; shader.uniforms.uSeeA = SEE.a; shader.uniforms.uSeeB = SEE.b; shader.uniforms.uSeeR = SEE.r; shader.uniforms.uSeeT = SEE.t1; shader.vertexShader = 'varying vec3 vSeeW;\n' + shader.vertexShader.replace('#include ', '#include \n#ifdef USE_INSTANCING\n vSeeW = (modelMatrix * instanceMatrix * vec4(transformed, 1.0)).xyz;\n#else\n vSeeW = (modelMatrix * vec4(transformed, 1.0)).xyz;\n#endif'); shader.fragmentShader = 'varying vec3 vSeeW;\nuniform float uSeeOn; uniform vec3 uSeeA; uniform vec3 uSeeB; uniform float uSeeR; uniform float uSeeT;\n' + shader.fragmentShader.replace('void main() {', 'void main() {\n if (uSeeOn > 0.5) { vec3 sAB = uSeeB - uSeeA; float sT = dot(vSeeW - uSeeA, sAB) / max(1e-4, dot(sAB, sAB)); if (sT > 0.0 && sT < uSeeT) { float sD = length(vSeeW - (uSeeA + sAB * sT)), sR = uSeeR * (0.3 + 0.7 * sT / uSeeT); if (sD < sR) { float sB = fract(52.9829189 * fract(dot(floor(gl_FragCoord.xy), vec2(0.06711056, 0.00583715)))); if (sB > mix(0.28, 1.0, smoothstep(0.7, 1.0, sD / sR))) discard; } } }'); }; THREE.Material.prototype.customProgramCacheKey = function () { return 'see1'; }; scene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xffffff, 0x7a7a52, 0.75)); var sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xfff3e0, 0.9); sun.position.set(12, 24, 8); sun.castShadow = true; sun.shadow.mapSize.set(2048, 2048); sun.shadow.camera.left = -26; sun.shadow.camera.right = 26; sun.shadow.camera.top = 26; sun.shadow.camera.bottom = -26; sun.shadow.camera.near = 1; sun.shadow.camera.far = 140; sun.shadow.bias = -0.0004; sun.shadow.normalBias = 0.05; scene.add(sun); scene.add(sun.target); // ---- Valley: rolling grass between two cliff walls and a back wall, running 3,750 tiles toward -Z, then a narrow curve climbing out ---- var VALLEY_TILES = 100; var LENGTH_TILES = 3750; // the valley ends in a cliff this far from the start var LEDGE_FREE_TILES = 2000; // no ledges until she has run this far var HALF = VALLEY_TILES * TILE / 2; // 100 units from the centre line to each side wall, and from the start to the back wall var GRID = 2.5; // size of one terrain facet var EXTENT = 150; // terrain reaches 150 to each side and 150 behind (the cliffs are 50 thick) var WALL_HEIGHT = 46; var END = LENGTH_TILES * TILE; // z = -END is the end wall var LEDGE_START = LEDGE_FREE_TILES * TILE; // Past the 7,500-unit mark the valley closes in and curves away. The walls pinch from 100 to 25 units either side of // centre at 45 degrees, then follow a 180-degree left-hand arc whose centre line has exactly the radius she can hold at top // speed (speed^2 / GRIP). The floor climbs along the arc until it is as high as the wall tops, and the channel opens // into a bare plateau. var FUN_LEN = 75, CH_HALF = 25; var TURN_R = RUN_SPEED * RUN_SPEED / GRIP; var FUN_Z1 = -END - FUN_LEN, ARC_CX = -TURN_R, ARC_CZ = FUN_Z1; var PL_X = ARC_CX - TURN_R, PL_HX = 25, PL_HZ = 30, PL_ZC = ARC_CZ + PL_HZ; // a short stub where the channel meets the plateau, so the floor carries on level (no apron) // The plateau is the high ground the valley is cut into. It runs from the valley's west wall (so the wall tops are its surface) // out to 5,000 units beyond the channel mouth, and from 600 past the arc's far side to 300 behind the start. Its other three // edges are cliffs into nothing, with an invisible barrier just short of each. // The plateau is a five-cornered shape: its east edge is the valley's west rim (x -100, z -7575 to -6800), its north edge runs west // along z -7575 (the line through the arc's centre) to x -3500, its west edge is 125 long (z -7575 to -7450), and its south edge // runs straight west along z -6800 as far as the mouth of the curve (x -1280) before tapering in to (-3500, -7450). The arc's own // rock keeps a rim round it, then drops away. var PX0 = -3500, PX1 = -HALF, PZ0 = ARC_CZ, PZ1 = -5950, PXW = -4300, PZ1_OLD = -6800, PZ_W = -7450; // bounding box; PZ1 is the south edge, PZ_W the low corner on the west edge var SL_DX = PX0 - PL_X, SL_DZ = PZ_W - PZ1_OLD, SL_LEN = Math.sqrt(SL_DX * SL_DX + SL_DZ * SL_DZ), SL_NX = SL_DZ / SL_LEN, SL_NZ = -SL_DX / SL_LEN; // SL_N points out over the tapering cliff var RIM_R = TURN_R + CH_HALF + 40; var pdN = 0, pdW = 0, pdE = 0, pdS = 0, pdF = 0, pdH = 0; // How far (x, z) lies outside the plateau (negative: inside), counting the rock rim round the arc as part of it. function plateauOut(x, z) { pdN = ARC_CZ - z; pdW = PXW - x; pdE = x - PX1; pdF = z - PZ1; pdS = -1e9; // the south edge now runs straight along the second valley's far rim (no taper) var dP = Math.max(pdN, pdW, pdE, pdF, pdS); pdH = z <= ARC_CZ ? Math.sqrt((x - ARC_CX) * (x - ARC_CX) + (z - ARC_CZ) * (z - ARC_CZ)) - RIM_R : 1e9; return Math.min(dP, pdH); } var PLATEAU_BARRIER = false; // the plateau's edges (and their row of short rock spikes) are open now; its east side never had one var HILL_MIN = 20, HILL_MAX = 100; var PLANE_Y = WALL_HEIGHT - 100, PLANE_LEN = 1500, PLANE_Z0 = -8500, PLANE_Z1 = -6100; // a flat plane 100 units below the plateau, west of its end var CHUNK = 100; // the world is built in square tiles this size, made ahead of her and dropped behind her var VIEW_AHEAD = 680 * GFX.fog, VIEW_BEHIND = 340; // how far from her the world is kept built (fog hides anything farther) // Smooth random field in [0,1] that never repeats (value noise on a lattice, hashed so it works at any distance) function vnoise(x, z, k) { var ix = Math.floor(x), iz = Math.floor(z), fx = x - ix, fz = z - iz; fx = fx * fx * (3 - 2 * fx); fz = fz * fz * (3 - 2 * fz); var a = rand01(ix, iz, k), b = rand01(ix + 1, iz, k), c = rand01(ix, iz + 1, k), d = rand01(ix + 1, iz + 1, k); return a + (b - a) * fx + (c - a) * fz + (a - b - c + d) * fx * fz; } function stepAt(n, t) { // 0 below the threshold, 1 above it, with a very short slope between var u = (n - t) / 0.0006 + 0.5; u = u < 0 ? 0 : u > 1 ? 1 : u; return u * u * (3 - 2 * u); } // Ledges: wherever a slow random field crosses a threshold the ground steps up by 0.5 to 3 units. The contour lines of // the field make irregular shelves, mesas and drops that come and go at random, none taller than she can jump. // How tall a ledge is at this spot: anywhere from half a unit to three, changing slowly along its length. var GEN_LEDGES = false; function ledgeStep(x, z, k) { var v = (vnoise(x / 75 + k * 3.1, z / 75 - k * 1.7, 20 + k) - 0.15) / 0.7; v = v < 0 ? 0 : v > 1 ? 1 : v; var hh = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.round(5 * v); var t = (-z - END * 0.4) / (END * 0.2); t = t < 0 ? 0 : t > 1 ? 1 : t; // in the second half of the valley every shelf grows to the full 3 units (and there are fewer of them, see ledgeH), so only a few big ridges are left return 0.5 * Math.round((hh + (3 - hh) * t) * 2); // in half-unit levels, so a shelf's top stays flat instead of stepping a hair every cell } function ledgeH(x, z) { if (x > L3_MIN) return sbLedge(x, z); if (x > 20000) return ovLedge(x, z); // (level 2: only the Sink's maze has any) if (!GEN_LEDGES) return 0; // Grass Canyon's generated ledges are switched off: ledges are placed by hand now if (x > HALF || x < -HALF) return 0; // ledges belong to the first valley alone (cells are sampled at their centres, so the cut falls exactly on its walls): none in the cave, the second valley or on the plateau var n = 0.65 * vnoise(x / 270, z / 270, 11) + 0.35 * vnoise(x / 118.5 + 5.3, z / 118.5 - 2.1, 12); // spread about 30% wider apart than before, so fewer ledges var q = (z + LEDGE_START) / -300; q = q < 0 ? 0 : q > 1 ? 1 : q; // none before LEDGE_START, then they come in over 300 units var e = (z + END) / 500; e = e < 0 ? 0 : e > 1 ? 1 : e; // and go out again over the last 500 units before the funnel var tl = (-z - END * 0.4) / (END * 0.2); tl = tl < 0 ? 0 : tl > 1 ? 1 : tl; var sh = (1 - q * q * (3 - 2 * q) * e * e * (3 - 2 * e)) * 0.75 + 0.18 * tl; // the fade moves the contour thresholds, so shelves open and close at their full height instead of shrinking into small, choppy steps var h = ledgeStep(x, z, 0) * stepAt(n, 0.55 + sh) + ledgeStep(x, z, 1) * stepAt(n, 0.68 + sh) + ledgeStep(x, z, 2) * stepAt(n, 0.32 + sh); var r = Math.sqrt(x * x + z * z); // the starting meadow stays flat var f = (r - 28) / 30; f = f < 0 ? 0 : f > 1 ? 1 : f; var m = (x + 600) / 200; m = m < 0 ? 0 : m > 1 ? 1 : m; // never out on the plateau return h * f * f * (3 - 2 * f) * m * m * (3 - 2 * m); } function terrainFlat(x, z) { return 0.95 * Math.sin(x * 0.045 + 0.7) * Math.cos(z * 0.037 - 0.4) + 0.60 * Math.sin((x + z) * 0.07 + 1.3) + 0.35 * Math.sin(x * 0.11 - z * 0.09 + 2.0); } function terrainH(x, z) { return terrainFlat(x, z) + ledgeH(x, z); } // Ledges are cut square: each 2.5-unit cell of the ground carries one ledge height (sampled at its centre), so a ledge // is a flat shelf with a vertical face where the next cell is a different height. function ledgeCell(ix, iz) { ledI = NaN; ledA = 0; var v = ledgeH((ix + 0.5) * GRID, (iz + 0.5) * GRID); ledCA = ledA; ledCI = ledI; return v; } // The height of the ground she actually stands on: the same flat triangles the mesh draws, so her feet never float or sink. var gcI = 1e9, gcJ = 1e9, gcA = 0, gcB = 0, gcC = 0, gcD = 0, ggx = 0, ggz = 0, gcBk = false, gcS = new Float64Array(36), BK_N = 5, ggxB = 0, ggzB = 0; // a cell beside a steep bank is cut into BK_N x BK_N pieces, the ground and the picture alike var lnH = 0, lnGX = 0, lnGZ = 0; function narY(p, ps) { var lf = p.lf; return p.y0 + (lf && lf.flat ? l2RiseG(ps, lf.sl0, lf.sl1, lf.E) : p.dy ? p.dy * smooth01(ps / p.len) : 0); } // the height of the narrow path's floor ps along piece p function narSl(p, ps) { // and how steeply it climbs there var lf = p.lf; if (lf && lf.flat) return ps < lf.E ? lf.sl0 + (lf.sl1 - lf.sl0) * ps / lf.E : lf.sl1; if (p.dy) { var t = ps / p.len; t = t < 0 ? 0 : t > 1 ? 1 : t; return p.dy * 6 * t * (1 - t) / p.len; } return 0; } function narHW(x, z) { // half the path's width: 4.6 between the rocks, opening out to 8 (16 wide) where it leaves the crags for the bay return L2_NHW + 3.4 * Math.max(smooth01((1330 - Math.hypot(x - L2_X, z + 741)) / 80), smooth01(ovKAt(x, z) / 0.15)); } function laneAt(x, z) { // (none in a bare Oceana) on the flat of the narrow path out of the cave? Then the ground is exactly the path's own height, not the mesh's (the mesh cannot keep a lane 6 wide perfectly smooth) if (L2_BARE) return false; var nd = l2Dist(x, z); if (L2_PI < L2_NAR0 || nd > narHW(x, z) + 0.3) return false; if (L2_PI === L2_PIECES.length - 1 && (x - L2_PIECES[L2_PI].x0) * L2_PIECES[L2_PI].hx + (z - L2_PIECES[L2_PI].z0) * L2_PIECES[L2_PI].hz > L2_PIECES[L2_PI].len) return false; // past the path's end there is no floor var p = L2_PIECES[L2_PI], sl = narSl(p, L2_PS); lnH = narY(p, L2_PS); if (sl) { var q = l2PointAt(L2_S); lnGX = sl * q[2]; lnGZ = sl * q[3]; } else { lnGX = 0; lnGZ = 0; } return true; } function groundH(x, z) { var h = groundH0(x, z); if (level === 3 && SB) { if (SB.movers.length) { var mt = sbMoverTop(x, z, sbMovPy(x, z)); if (mt > h) h = mt; } if (SB.slabH) { var sst = sbSlabTop(x, z, sbMovPy(x, z)); if (sst > h) h = sst; } // a floating slab of path if (SB.terrain === 'level2') { var dkt = sbDeckTop(x, z, sbMovPy(x, z)); if (dkt > h) h = dkt; } // Oceana: the path's own deck, unless the ground stands higher than it here if (SB.caveZone && player.position.x - x < 8 && x - player.position.x < 8 && player.position.z - z < 8 && z - player.position.z < 8) { // inside a tunnel (or its mouth) her ground is the tunnel's floor var cq = sbCaveQ(SB.caveZone, x, z), py = player.position.y; if (cq && py < cq.rf + 0.2 && py >= cq.fl - 4) return cq.fl; } if (SB.shafts.length) { var shf = sbShaftIn(x, z, 0); if (shf) return shf.floor; } // inside a shaft's outline the ground is its floor, and the wall is exactly the outline } return h; } function sbLane(x, z) { return laneAt(L2_X + x - L3_X, z + L2_SBZ); } // Sandbox, Oceana Crags' ground: the narrow path out of the cave is the real level's own lane, exactly as it is there function groundH0(x, z) { if (level === 2 && x > L2_MIN && laneAt(x, z)) return lnH; if (level === 3 && SB && SB.terrain === 'level2' && sbLane(x, z)) return lnH; var ix = Math.floor(x / GRID), iz = Math.floor(z / GRID), gx = ix * GRID, gz = iz * GRID; if (ix !== gcI || iz !== gcJ) { var L = ledgeCell(ix, iz), La = ledCA, LI = ledCI; l3Bk = 0; gcA = cornerY(gx, gz, L, La, LI); var bk0 = l3Bk; l3Bk = 0; gcB = cornerY(gx + GRID, gz, L, La, LI); bk0 = bk0 || l3Bk; l3Bk = 0; gcC = cornerY(gx, gz + GRID, L, La, LI); bk0 = bk0 || l3Bk; l3Bk = 0; gcD = cornerY(gx + GRID, gz + GRID, L, La, LI); bk0 = bk0 || l3Bk; gcBk = !!bk0 && level === 3; if (gcBk) for (var sj = 0; sj <= BK_N; sj++) for (var si = 0; si <= BK_N; si++) gcS[sj * (BK_N + 1) + si] = cornerY(gx + si * GRID / BK_N, gz + sj * GRID / BK_N, L, La, LI); gcI = ix; gcJ = iz; } if (gcBk) { var su = (x - gx) / GRID * BK_N, sv = (z - gz) / GRID * BK_N, bi = Math.min(BK_N - 1, Math.max(0, Math.floor(su))), bj = Math.min(BK_N - 1, Math.max(0, Math.floor(sv))), fu = su - bi, fv = sv - bj, W6 = BK_N + 1, hs = GRID / BK_N; var hA = gcS[bj * W6 + bi], hB = gcS[bj * W6 + bi + 1], hC = gcS[(bj + 1) * W6 + bi], hD = gcS[(bj + 1) * W6 + bi + 1]; if (fu + fv <= 1) { ggxB = (hB - hA) / hs; ggzB = (hC - hA) / hs; return hA + fu * (hB - hA) + fv * (hC - hA); } ggxB = (hD - hC) / hs; ggzB = (hD - hB) / hs; return hD + (1 - fu) * (hC - hD) + (1 - fv) * (hB - hD); } var u = (x - gx) / GRID, v = (z - gz) / GRID; if (u + v <= 1) return gcA + u * (gcB - gcA) + v * (gcC - gcA); return gcD + (1 - u) * (gcC - gcD) + (1 - v) * (gcB - gcD); } // The slope of the very triangle she stands on (ggx, ggz = rise per unit run), so the foot of a ledge face is not mistaken for a hillside. var ggPath = false; // set by groundGrad: the slope came from a running surface (a lane, deck, slab, platform, banked path, shaft or cave floor), not the open ground function groundGrad(x, z) { ggPath = true; if (level === 2 && x > L2_MIN && laneAt(x, z)) { ggx = lnGX; ggz = lnGZ; return; } if (level === 3 && SB && SB.terrain === 'level2' && sbLane(x, z)) { ggx = lnGX; ggz = lnGZ; return; } if (level === 3 && SB && SB.movers.length && sbMoverTop(x, z, sbMovPy(x, z)) > groundH0(x, z)) { ggx = 0; ggz = 0; return; } if (level === 3 && SB && SB.terrain === 'level2') { var dpy = sbMovPy(x, z), dk0 = sbDeckTop(x, z, dpy); // on one of Oceana's path decks: the deck's own slope, from points on it if (dk0 > groundH0(x, z)) { var dd = 0.4, da1 = sbDeckTop(x + dd, z, dpy), da2 = sbDeckTop(x - dd, z, dpy), db1 = sbDeckTop(x, z + dd, dpy), db2 = sbDeckTop(x, z - dd, dpy); ggx = da1 > -1e8 && da2 > -1e8 ? (da1 - da2) / (2 * dd) : da1 > -1e8 ? (da1 - dk0) / dd : da2 > -1e8 ? (dk0 - da2) / dd : 0; ggz = db1 > -1e8 && db2 > -1e8 ? (db1 - db2) / (2 * dd) : db1 > -1e8 ? (db1 - dk0) / dd : db2 > -1e8 ? (dk0 - db2) / dd : 0; return; } } if (level === 3 && SB && SB.slabH) { var spy = sbMovPy(x, z), st0 = sbSlabTop(x, z, spy); if (st0 > groundH0(x, z)) { var sd = 0.4, sa1 = sbSlabTop(x + sd, z, spy), sa2 = sbSlabTop(x - sd, z, spy), sb1 = sbSlabTop(x, z + sd, spy), sb2 = sbSlabTop(x, z - sd, spy); ggx = sa1 > -1e8 && sa2 > -1e8 ? (sa1 - sa2) / (2 * sd) : 0; ggz = sb1 > -1e8 && sb2 > -1e8 ? (sb1 - sb2) / (2 * sd) : 0; return; } } if (level === 3 && SB && SB.caveZone) { var cq2 = sbCaveQ(SB.caveZone, x, z), py2 = player.position.y; if (cq2 && py2 < cq2.rf + 0.2 && py2 >= cq2.fl - 4) { var ce2 = cq2.e, la = Math.max(-ce2.hw, Math.min(ce2.hw, cq2.lat)), ra = polyRange(ce2.pts, Math.max(-ce2.hw, la - 0.3)), rb = polyRange(ce2.pts, Math.min(ce2.hw, la + 0.3)), dl = 0; // the floor drops along the tunnel as it descends, and tilts sideways in a round or pointed cross-section if (ra && rb) dl = (rb[0] - ra[0]) / ce2.cp / Math.max(0.01, Math.min(ce2.hw, la + 0.3) - Math.max(-ce2.hw, la - 0.3)); ggx = -ce2.Thx * ce2.tn + ce2.Lx * dl; ggz = -ce2.Thz * ce2.tn + ce2.Lz * dl; return; } } if (level === 3 && SB && SB.shafts.length && sbShaftIn(x, z, 0)) { ggx = 0; ggz = 0; return; } groundH0(x, z); if (gcBk) { ggx = ggxB; ggz = ggzB; return; } ggPath = false; var gx = Math.floor(x / GRID) * GRID, gz = Math.floor(z / GRID) * GRID; if ((x - gx) / GRID + (z - gz) / GRID <= 1) { ggx = (gcB - gcA) / GRID; ggz = (gcC - gcA) / GRID; } else { ggx = (gcD - gcC) / GRID; ggz = (gcD - gcB) / GRID; } } // How far (x, z) is inside the rock (negative: open ground), the floor height there, and how tall the walls beside it // stand above that floor. The open ground is the union of four shapes: the valley, the funnel, the arc and the plateau. var WALL_RAISE = 100; // the first valley's side walls (and its back wall) are 100 taller than the plateau, down to z -6700 var wd = 0, wF = 0, wR = 0; function smooth01(t) { t = t < 0 ? 0 : t > 1 ? 1 : t; return t * t * (3 - 2 * t); } function wallField(x, z) { var ax = Math.abs(x); var d1 = Math.max(ax - HALF, z - HALF, -END - z); var hw = CH_HALF + (z - FUN_Z1); hw = hw < CH_HALF ? CH_HALF : hw > HALF ? HALF : hw; var d2 = Math.max((ax - hw) * 0.70711, FUN_Z1 - z, z + END); var vx = x - ARC_CX, vz = z - ARC_CZ, d3 = 1e6, ringF = 0; if (vz <= 0) { d3 = Math.abs(Math.sqrt(vx * vx + vz * vz) - TURN_R) - CH_HALF; ringF = WALL_HEIGHT * smooth01(Math.atan2(0 - vz, vx) / Math.PI); // the floor climbs smoothly along the whole turn } var d4 = Math.max(Math.abs(x - PL_X) - PL_HX, Math.abs(z - PL_ZC) - PL_HZ); var dm = Math.min(d1, d2, d3, d4); var w1 = Math.exp((dm - d1) / 8), w2 = Math.exp((dm - d2) / 8), w3 = Math.exp((dm - d3) / 8), w4 = Math.exp((dm - d4) / 8); var sw = w1 + w2 + w3 + w4; wd = dm; wF = (w3 * ringF + w4 * WALL_HEIGHT) / sw; wR = ((w1 + w2) * WALL_HEIGHT + w3 * (WALL_HEIGHT - ringF)) / sw + WALL_RAISE * (w1 / sw) * (x < 0 ? 1 - smooth01((dm - 45) / 50) : 1) * (z >= -6700 ? 1 : z <= -6800 ? 0 : 1 - smooth01((-6700 - z) / 100)); // the valley's two walls stand 100 higher, back to where the plateau begins // along the arc the rim stays level while the floor rises to meet it; the apron has no walls } // Mounds on the plateau: round, shallow domes, as tall at every side of the base and highest in the middle. They are 7 to 25 units // tall and as wide as it takes to keep every slope under what she can climb (the wider the taller). Each 300-unit cell holds at // most one, wholly inside the cell, so they never overlap. They keep clear of the canyons, the apron, the arc, the cliffs and // the second checkpoint. var MOUND_CELL = 240, moundCache = new Map(); function chainDist(x, z) { // distance to the Seg chain's arc (radius 640, 68 degrees from the mouth), so no mound sits on it var ccx = PL_X - 640, ccz = ARC_CZ + 30, a = Math.atan2(z - ccz, x - ccx); a = a < 0 ? 0 : a > 1.19 ? 1.19 : a; return Math.hypot(x - (ccx + 640 * Math.cos(a)), z - (ccz + 640 * Math.sin(a))); } function moundAt(ci, cj) { var key = cj * 8192 + ci + 4096, m = moundCache.get(key); if (m !== undefined) return m; m = null; var rnd = cellRng(ci + 9000, cj - 9000); var occ = rnd(), hh0 = 7 + 18 * rnd(), wk = 4.2 + 1.3 * rnd(); if (occ < 0.95) for (var tryN = 0; tryN < 14 && m === null; tryN++) { // if it will not fit here, try another spot and a smaller one var hh = Math.max(5, hh0 * (1 - 0.07 * tryN)), r = hh * wk; if (r > MOUND_CELL / 2 - 1) { r = MOUND_CELL / 2 - 1; hh = r / wk; } // a dome never reaches past its own cell // steepest slope hh * pi / (2r) is at most 0.37 var cx = (ci + 0.5) * MOUND_CELL + (rnd() - 0.5) * (MOUND_CELL - 2 * r - 2), cz = (cj + 0.5) * MOUND_CELL + (rnd() - 0.5) * (MOUND_CELL - 2 * r - 2); var g2x = cx - CP2_X, g2z = cz - CP2_Z; if (plateauOut(cx, cz) < -(r + 10) && cz > ARC_CZ + r + 10 && cx < PX1 - r - 40 && Math.max(Math.abs(cx - PL_X) - PL_HX, Math.abs(cz - PL_ZC) - PL_HZ) > r + 10 && g2x * g2x + g2z * g2z > (r + 60) * (r + 60) && chainDist(cx, cz) > r + 14 && valley2Dist(cx, cz) > V2_HALF + V2_RIM + r + 10) { m = { x: cx, z: cz, r: r, h: hh }; } } moundCache.set(key, m); return m; } // A second, finer layer of small mounds (5 to 11 units tall) fills the gaps between the big ones: one per 120-unit cell at most, // kept clear of every big mound and of everything the big ones keep clear of. var MOUND_CELL_B = 120, moundCacheB = new Map(); function moundBAt(ci, cj) { var key = cj * 8192 + ci + 4096, m = moundCacheB.get(key); if (m !== undefined) return m; m = null; var rnd = cellRng(ci + 17000, cj - 17000); var occ = rnd(), hh0 = 5 + 6 * rnd(), wk = 4.2 + 1.3 * rnd(); if (occ < 0.9) for (var tryN = 0; tryN < 8 && m === null; tryN++) { var hh = Math.max(4, hh0 * (1 - 0.08 * tryN)), r = hh * wk; if (r > MOUND_CELL_B / 2 - 1) { r = MOUND_CELL_B / 2 - 1; hh = r / wk; } var cx = (ci + 0.5) * MOUND_CELL_B + (rnd() - 0.5) * (MOUND_CELL_B - 2 * r - 2), cz = (cj + 0.5) * MOUND_CELL_B + (rnd() - 0.5) * (MOUND_CELL_B - 2 * r - 2); var g2x = cx - CP2_X, g2z = cz - CP2_Z; if (plateauOut(cx, cz) < -(r + 10) && cz > ARC_CZ + r + 10 && cx < PX1 - r - 40 && Math.max(Math.abs(cx - PL_X) - PL_HX, Math.abs(cz - PL_ZC) - PL_HZ) > r + 10 && g2x * g2x + g2z * g2z > (r + 60) * (r + 60) && chainDist(cx, cz) > r + 14 && valley2Dist(cx, cz) > V2_HALF + V2_RIM + r + 10) { var clear = true, ca = Math.floor(cx / MOUND_CELL), cb = Math.floor(cz / MOUND_CELL); for (var da = -1; da <= 1 && clear; da++) for (var db = -1; db <= 1; db++) { var big = moundAt(ca + da, cb + db); if (big !== null && Math.hypot(big.x - cx, big.z - cz) < big.r + r + 8) { clear = false; break; } } if (clear) m = { x: cx, z: cz, r: r, h: hh }; } } moundCacheB.set(key, m); return m; } function domeAt(m, x, z) { if (m === null) return 0; var dx = x - m.x, dz = z - m.z, d2 = dx * dx + dz * dz; if (d2 >= m.r * m.r) return 0; return m.h * 0.5 * (1 + Math.cos(Math.PI * Math.sqrt(d2) / m.r)); // a smooth dome: level at the rim, flat on top } function hillH(x, z) { return domeAt(moundAt(Math.floor(x / MOUND_CELL), Math.floor(z / MOUND_CELL)), x, z) + domeAt(moundBAt(Math.floor(x / MOUND_CELL_B), Math.floor(z / MOUND_CELL_B)), x, z); } // The ramp is straight, 125 wide like the plateau's end, along -X. Its slope eases up from level to 45 degrees over 50 units, holds // 45 degrees for 350, and eases back to level over 150 more, onto a flat square platform 200 on a side. var RMP_L1 = 50, RMP_L2 = 575, RMP_L3 = 50, RMP_END = RMP_L1 + RMP_L2 + RMP_L3, RMP_PLAT = 200, RMP_W = 125, RMP_ZC = -7512.5, RMP_A = Math.PI / 4; var RMP_Y1 = RMP_L1 / RMP_A * -Math.log(Math.cos(RMP_A)), RMP_Y2 = RMP_Y1 + RMP_L2, RMP_H = RMP_Y2 + RMP_L3 / RMP_A * -Math.log(Math.cos(RMP_A)); function rampRise(s) { // height above the plateau at distance s along the ramp if (s <= 0) return 0; if (s < RMP_L1) return RMP_L1 / RMP_A * -Math.log(Math.cos(RMP_A * s / RMP_L1)); if (s < RMP_L1 + RMP_L2) return RMP_Y1 + (s - RMP_L1); if (s < RMP_END) { var t = s - RMP_L1 - RMP_L2; return RMP_Y2 + RMP_L3 / RMP_A * (Math.log(Math.cos(RMP_A * (1 - t / RMP_L3))) - Math.log(Math.cos(RMP_A))); } return RMP_H; } // The second valley winds away west of the platform: 60 wide, its floor 100 below the platform, its walls cut square and flush with the // platform on the east side. Its centre line is a 500-unit straight run along +Z, then four 350-radius turns (right 90, left 90, right 180, // left 90, so it winds east), a further 45° left, and then a gentler 45° right back to due east while the floor eases down to the level of the // first valley's old wall-top level (y 46); its walls stay 100 tall, meeting the raised west wall of the first valley (x -100) as a notch through it. // An 80-unit rim of level ground lies on each side before the world falls away. var V2_D = 0, V2_WI = 22, V2_WO = 38; // the wall rises in an S-curve between these distances from the centre line (the old square wall stood at 30) var V2_HALF = 30, V2_RIM = 80, V2_R = 350, V2_PIECES = [], V2_BOX = [1e9, -1e9, 1e9, -1e9], V2_LEN = 0, V2_DESC = 0, V2_XEND = -100, V2_S = 0, V2_FLOOR = false, V2_ZEND = 0; (function () { var px = -4405, pz = -7612.5, hx = 0, hz = 1, S = 0; function note(x, z) { V2_BOX[0] = Math.min(V2_BOX[0], x); V2_BOX[1] = Math.max(V2_BOX[1], x); V2_BOX[2] = Math.min(V2_BOX[2], z); V2_BOX[3] = Math.max(V2_BOX[3], z); } function straight(len) { V2_PIECES.push({ t: 0, x0: px, z0: pz, x1: px + hx * len, z1: pz + hz * len, s0: S, len: len }); px += hx * len; pz += hz * len; S += len; note(px, pz); } function arc(side, ang, R) { // side +1 turns left of the heading (-hz, hx), -1 right var nx = side * -hz, nz = side * hx, cx = px + nx * R, cz = pz + nz * R; var a0 = Math.atan2(pz - cz, px - cx), a1 = a0 + side * ang; V2_PIECES.push({ t: 1, cx: cx, cz: cz, R: R, side: side, a0: Math.min(a0, a1), a1: Math.max(a0, a1), ex0: px, ez0: pz, s0: S, len: R * ang }); px = cx + R * Math.cos(a1); pz = cz + R * Math.sin(a1); S += R * ang; hx = side > 0 ? -Math.sin(a1) : Math.sin(a1); hz = side > 0 ? Math.cos(a1) : -Math.cos(a1); V2_PIECES[V2_PIECES.length - 1].ex1 = px; V2_PIECES[V2_PIECES.length - 1].ez1 = pz; for (var q = 0; q <= 8; q++) { var aa = a0 + (a1 - a0) * q / 8; note(cx + R * Math.cos(aa), cz + R * Math.sin(aa)); } } note(px, pz); straight(500); arc(-1, Math.PI / 2, V2_R); arc(1, Math.PI / 2, V2_R); arc(-1, Math.PI, V2_R); arc(1, Math.PI / 2, V2_R); arc(1, Math.PI / 4, V2_R); // 45° toward +X,+Z V2_DESC = S; // the floor starts to descend here arc(-1, Math.PI / 4, 1000); // a gentler curve back to due east straight(V2_XEND - px); // east to the first valley's west wall V2_LEN = S; V2_ZEND = pz; })(); var PATH_S = 0; function pathDist(P, x, z) { // distance from (x, z) to a centre line made of straights and arcs (PATH_S gets the path length there) var d = 1e9, i, c, t, dx, dz, e, sp = 0; for (i = 0; i < P.length; i++) { c = P[i]; if (c.t === 0) { dx = c.x1 - c.x0; dz = c.z1 - c.z0; t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((x - c.x0) * dx + (z - c.z0) * dz) / (dx * dx + dz * dz))); e = Math.hypot(x - c.x0 - dx * t, z - c.z0 - dz * t); if (e < d) { d = e; sp = c.s0 + t * c.len; } } else { var ang = Math.atan2(z - c.cz, x - c.cx); while (ang < c.a0 - 1e-9) ang += 2 * Math.PI; while (ang > c.a0 + 2 * Math.PI) ang -= 2 * Math.PI; if (ang <= c.a1 + 1e-9) { e = Math.abs(Math.hypot(x - c.cx, z - c.cz) - c.R); if (e < d) { d = e; sp = c.s0 + c.R * (c.side > 0 ? ang - c.a0 : c.a1 - ang); } } else { var e0 = Math.hypot(x - c.ex0, z - c.ez0), e1 = Math.hypot(x - c.ex1, z - c.ez1); e = Math.min(e0, e1); if (e < d) { d = e; sp = e0 < e1 ? c.s0 : c.s0 + c.len; } } } } PATH_S = sp; return d; } function valley2Dist(x, z) { var d = pathDist(V2_PIECES, x, z); V2_S = PATH_S; return d; } function v2Floor(s) { // floor height along the path: level, then a long eased descent to the first valley's wall-top level var top = WALL_HEIGHT + rampRise(RMP_END), drop = top - 100 - WALL_HEIGHT; if (s <= V2_DESC) return top - 100; var u = Math.min(1, (s - V2_DESC) / (V2_LEN - V2_DESC)), ee = 0.12, m = 1 / (1 - ee), g; if (u < ee) g = m * u * u / (2 * ee); else if (u < 1 - ee) g = m * (ee / 2 + u - ee); else g = 1 - m * (1 - u) * (1 - u) / (2 * ee); return top - 100 - drop * g; } function valley2(x, z) { // its height at (x, z), or null away from it if (x >= V2_XEND || x < V2_BOX[0] - V2_HALF - V2_RIM || x > V2_BOX[1] + V2_HALF + V2_RIM || z < V2_BOX[2] - V2_HALF - V2_RIM || z > V2_BOX[3] + V2_HALF + V2_RIM) return null; var d = valley2Dist(x, z); if (d >= V2_HALF + V2_RIM) return null; V2_FLOOR = d < V2_WI + 2; V2_D = d; var f = v2Floor(V2_S), base = f + terrainFlat(x, z) * (1 - smooth01((V2_S - V2_DESC) / 300)); if (d <= V2_WI) return base; if (d < V2_WO) { var u = (d - V2_WI) / (V2_WO - V2_WI); return base + (f + 100 - base) * u * u * (3 - 2 * u); } // an S-curve from the floor up to the rim, so neither the foot nor the lip of the wall is a hard corner return f + 100; // its walls stay 100 tall, so at the end they are flush with the first valley's raised wall } // ---- The cave ---- // A 30-wide, 30-tall tunnel enters the first valley's raised east wall from the notch where the second valley ends (same floor height, y 46): // 100 units straight east, then a 450-radius left turn to -Z, then 800 straight on, climbing at 22.5 degrees from the start of the turn so she // loses 50 Zips before it levels out, and ending in a round chamber 100 in radius and 60 tall. It lies wholly east of the valley, so no part of // the course is over or under it. The ground is a height map, so the tunnel floor is the ground and the roof is a separate lid (see below). var CAVE_W = 30, CAVE_H = 30, CAVE_R = 450, CAVE_X0 = HALF, CAVE_SLAB = 150, CH_R = 100, CH_H = 60, CAVE_BAND = 55; var CAVE_S2 = 800, CAVE_PIECES = [], CAVE_LEN = 0, CAVE_CLIMB0 = 100, CAVE_CL1 = 50, CAVE_CL2 = 1400, CAVE_CL3 = 50, CAVE_Z0 = V2_ZEND, CAVE_XE = 0, CAVE_ZE = 0, CH_X = 0, CH_Z = 0, CH_FLOOR = 0; (function () { var px = CAVE_X0, pz = CAVE_Z0, hx = 1, hz = 0, S = 0; function straight(len) { CAVE_PIECES.push({ t: 0, x0: px, z0: pz, x1: px + hx * len, z1: pz + hz * len, s0: S, len: len }); px += hx * len; pz += hz * len; S += len; } function arc(side, ang, R) { var nx = side * -hz, nz = side * hx, cx = px + nx * R, cz = pz + nz * R; var a0 = Math.atan2(pz - cz, px - cx), a1 = a0 + side * ang; CAVE_PIECES.push({ t: 1, cx: cx, cz: cz, R: R, side: side, a0: Math.min(a0, a1), a1: Math.max(a0, a1), ex0: px, ez0: pz, s0: S, len: R * ang }); px = cx + R * Math.cos(a1); pz = cz + R * Math.sin(a1); S += R * ang; hx = side > 0 ? -Math.sin(a1) : Math.sin(a1); hz = side > 0 ? Math.cos(a1) : -Math.cos(a1); CAVE_PIECES[CAVE_PIECES.length - 1].ex1 = px; CAVE_PIECES[CAVE_PIECES.length - 1].ez1 = pz; } straight(100); arc(-1, Math.PI / 2, CAVE_R); straight(CAVE_S2); CAVE_LEN = S; CAVE_XE = px; CAVE_ZE = pz; CH_X = px; CH_Z = pz + hz * CH_R; })(); var CAVE_A = Math.PI / 8, CAVE_Y1 = CAVE_CL1 / CAVE_A * -Math.log(Math.cos(CAVE_A)); function caveRise(t) { // height above the entrance floor at distance t into the climb: eases up to 22.5 degrees, holds, eases back to level if (t <= 0) return 0; if (t < CAVE_CL1) return CAVE_CL1 / CAVE_A * -Math.log(Math.cos(CAVE_A * t / CAVE_CL1)); if (t < CAVE_CL1 + CAVE_CL2) return CAVE_Y1 + (t - CAVE_CL1) * Math.tan(CAVE_A); var y2 = CAVE_Y1 + CAVE_CL2 * Math.tan(CAVE_A); if (t < CAVE_CL1 + CAVE_CL2 + CAVE_CL3) { var u = t - CAVE_CL1 - CAVE_CL2; return y2 + CAVE_CL3 / CAVE_A * (Math.log(Math.cos(CAVE_A * (1 - u / CAVE_CL3))) - Math.log(Math.cos(CAVE_A))); } return y2 + CAVE_Y1; } function caveFloor(s) { return WALL_HEIGHT + caveRise(s - CAVE_CLIMB0); } CH_FLOOR = caveFloor(1e9); // ---- The doorway, the mud ramp and the field ---- // A 10 x 10 doorway in the chamber's east wall, 6 above its floor (just a jump). It is sealed until all the mobs are down. Inside: // 65 straight east, a sharp 90-degree left turn (radius 8) to -Z, then a 200-long ramp at 45 degrees (10 easing in, 180 steady, 10 easing out) // paved in mud, ending at the cave's other mouth, which opens onto a grassy field. The goalpost stands 100 units out across the field. var DR_W = 10, DR_H = 10, DR_UP = 6, DR_S1 = 65, DR_TR = 8, DR_BAND = 25, DR_PIECES = [], DR_LEN = 0, DR_X0 = CH_X + CH_R, DR_Z0 = CH_Z, DR_Y0 = CH_FLOOR + DR_UP; var DR_CL1 = 10, DR_CL2 = 180, DR_CL3 = 10, DR_A = Math.PI / 4, DR_CLIMB0 = 0, DR_XE = 0, DR_ZE = 0, DR_TOPX = 0; var FIELD_HW = 60, FIELD_LEN = 560, GOAL_DIST = 500, GOAL_HALF = 20, FIELD_DIP = 1.5; // the field lies 1.5 below the top of the mud ramp, a sheer step she shoots off at whatever speed she has (function () { var px = DR_X0, pz = DR_Z0, hx = 1, hz = 0, S = 0; function straight(len) { DR_PIECES.push({ t: 0, x0: px, z0: pz, x1: px + hx * len, z1: pz + hz * len, s0: S, len: len }); px += hx * len; pz += hz * len; S += len; } function arc(side, ang, R) { var nx = side * -hz, nz = side * hx, cx = px + nx * R, cz = pz + nz * R; var a0 = Math.atan2(pz - cz, px - cx), a1 = a0 + side * ang; DR_PIECES.push({ t: 1, cx: cx, cz: cz, R: R, side: side, a0: Math.min(a0, a1), a1: Math.max(a0, a1), ex0: px, ez0: pz, s0: S, len: R * ang }); px = cx + R * Math.cos(a1); pz = cz + R * Math.sin(a1); S += R * ang; hx = side > 0 ? -Math.sin(a1) : Math.sin(a1); hz = side > 0 ? if (x > L3_MIN) return sbMudAt(x, z); if (x < DR_XE - DR_W || x > DR_XE + DR_W || z > DR_ZE + DR_CL1 + DR_CL2 + DR_CL3 + 2 || z < DR_ZE - 1) return false; var d = pathDist(DR_PIECES, x, z); return d < DR_W / 2 + 0.5 && PATH_S >= DR_CLIMB0 && PATH_S <= DR_LEN; } var cK = 1; // set by cave: what the spot is made of, 1 rock, 2 grass, 3 mud function cave(x, z) { // its height at (x, z), or null away from it cK = 1; var inDr = x >= DR_X0 && x <= DR_XE + FIELD_HW + 4 && z <= DR_Z0 + DR_BAND + 4 && z >= DR_ZE - FIELD_LEN - 4, dd = 0, ds = 0; if (inDr) { if (z <= DR_ZE && z >= DR_ZE - FIELD_LEN && Math.abs(x - DR_XE) <= FIELD_HW) { cK = 2; return FIELD_Y - FIELD_DIP; } // the grassy field dd = pathDist(DR_PIECES, x, z); ds = PATH_S; if (dd < DR_W / 2 && z > DR_ZE) { cK = ds >= DR_CLIMB0 ? 3 : 1; return drFloor(ds); } // the doorway passage, and the mud ramp } if (x < CAVE_X0 || x > CH_X + CH_R + 60 || z > CAVE_Z0 + CAVE_BAND + 5 || z < CH_Z - CH_R - 60) { if (inDr && dd < DR_BAND && z > DR_ZE && Math.hypot(x - CH_X, z - CH_Z) >= CH_R + 50) return drFloor(ds) + DR_H; // rock either side of the ramp, as high as its roof return null; } var d = pathDist(CAVE_PIECES, x, z), s = PATH_S, dc = Math.hypot(x - CH_X, z - CH_Z); if (d < CAVE_W / 2) return caveFloor(s); // the tunnel floor (also through the raised wall) if (dc < CH_R) return CH_FLOOR; // the chamber floor if (dc < CH_R + 50) return CH_FLOOR + CH_H; // its rock rim if (inDr && dd < DR_BAND && z > DR_ZE) return drFloor(ds) + DR_H; if (d < CAVE_BAND && x >= CAVE_SLAB) return caveFloor(s) + CAVE_H; // rock either side of the tunnel, as high as its roof return null; } function cavePoint(s) { return piecePoint(CAVE_PIECES, s); } function piecePoint(PP, s) { // [x, z, heading x, heading z] on a tunnel's centre line for (var i = 0; i < PP.length; i++) { var c = PP[i]; if (s <= c.s0 + c.len + 1e-6 || i === PP.length - 1) { var u = Math.max(0, Math.min(c.len, s - c.s0)); if (c.t === 0) { var f = u / c.len; return [c.x0 + (c.x1 - c.x0) * f, c.z0 + (c.z1 - c.z0) * f, (c.x1 - c.x0) / c.len, (c.z1 - c.z0) / c.len]; } var a = (c.side > 0 ? c.a0 : c.a1) + c.side * u / c.R; return [c.cx + c.R * Math.cos(a), c.cz + c.R * Math.sin(a), c.side > 0 ? -Math.sin(a) : Math.sin(a), c.side > 0 ? Math.cos(a) : -Math.cos(a)]; } } } var wMud = 0, wL2 = 0; // ---- Level 2, Oceana Crags: a flat, light brown stone runway 20 wide running -Z from a start pad, with deep water 10 below its edge. It lies // far east of everything in level 1 (which ends near x 1100), so none of level 1's tips, trees, Segs or gates are anywhere near it. ---- var L2_X = 30000, L2_MIN = 20000, L2_HW = 10, L2_Z0 = 40, L2_SBZ = 0, L2_LEN = 30, L2_Y = 40, WATER_Y = L2_Y - 10, L2_VOID = -300; // The runway is a centre line of pieces: 2,100 straight (the start pad and 2,000 of runway), a shallow 45 degree left (west) turn of radius 800, // a 135 degree right turn of the same radius that leaves her heading due east, a 200 straightaway, then a short ramp at 30 degrees. var L2_PIECES = [], L2_BOX = [1e9, -1e9, 1e9, -1e9], L2_S = 0, L2_PS = 0, L2_PI = 0, L2_STRAIGHT0 = 0, L2_RAMP0 = 0, L2_RAMP_L = 45, L2_RAMP_T = Math.tan(Math.PI / 6), L2_EASE = 6, L2_STYLE_ZONE = 15, L2_GAP = 300, L2_LAND1 = 200, L2_GAPX = 2025, L2_CURVE_R = 150, L2_CURVE_UP = 15, L2_CLIMB_TURN = 79.7, L2_PEAK_X = 2048, L2_PEAK_Z = -1697, L2_PEAK_Y = 125, L2_CLIMB_I = 0, L2_CLIMB_SL = 0, L2_CAVE_Z = -1404, L2_CAVE_Y = 107, L2_CAVE_L = 100, L2_CAVE_I = 0, L2_CAVE_GAP0 = 8, L2_RAMP_I = 0, L2_BAYEND_I = 0, L2_LAND1_I = 0, L2_RAMP2_I = 0, L2_LAND2_I = 0; // The way out of the cave: a narrow path (6 wide) down a 30 degree slope to ground level, then 2,000 units on between walls of rock. L2_NSEGS lists its bends as [length, turn in radians (+ left)]. var L2_NAR0 = 0, L2_FLAT0 = 0, L2_NHW = 4.6, L2_NLIM = 4.4, L2_NSEGS = [[205.4652, -0.684884], [1633.8518, 0]]; (function () { var px = L2_X, pz = L2_Z0, hx = 0, hz = -1, s = 0, curY = L2_Y; function note0() {} function note() { L2_BOX[0] = Math.min(L2_BOX[0], px - 40); L2_BOX[1] = Math.max(L2_BOX[1], px + 40); L2_BOX[2] = Math.min(L2_BOX[2], pz - 40); L2_BOX[3] = Math.max(L2_BOX[3], pz + 40); } function straight(len, sqStart, sqEnd, ramp, lf) { L2_PIECES.push({ t: 's', x0: px, z0: pz, hx: hx, hz: hz, len: len, s0: s, sqStart: !!sqStart, sqEnd: !!sqEnd, ramp: !!ramp, y0: curY, dy: 0, lf: lf || null, b0: lf ? lf.b0 : 0, blend: !!(lf && lf.blend) }); if (lf && !lf.keepY) curY += l2RiseG(len, lf.sl0, lf.sl1, lf.E); note(); px += hx * len; pz += hz * len; s += len; note(); } function arc(sig, deg, Rr, dy) { // sig +1 turns left (toward west when heading -Z), -1 turns right var R = Rr || 800, th = deg * Math.PI / 180, cx = px + sig * R * hz, cz = pz - sig * R * hx, ux = px - cx, uz = pz - cz; L2_PIECES.push({ t: 'a', cx: cx, cz: cz, ux: ux, uz: uz, R: R, th: th, sig: sig, s0: s, len: R * th, y0: curY, dy: dy || 0 }); curY += dy || 0; for (var k = 1; k <= 24; k++) { var f = sig * th * k / 24, c = Math.cos(f), sn = Math.sin(f); px = cx + ux * c + uz * sn; pz = cz - ux * sn + uz * c; note(); } var f2 = sig * th, c2 = Math.cos(f2), s2 = Math.sin(f2), nhx = hx * c2 + hz * s2, nhz = -hx * s2 + hz * c2; hx = nhx; hz = nhz; s += R * th; } straight(L2_Z0 + L2_LEN, true, false); arc(1, 45); arc(-1, 135); L2_STRAIGHT0 = s; straight(200); L2_RAMP0 = s; straight(L2_RAMP_L, false, true, true); L2_RAMP_I = L2_PIECES.length - 1; px += hx * L2_GAP; pz += hz * L2_GAP; s += L2_GAP; L2_LAND1_I = L2_PIECES.length; straight(L2_LAND1, true, false); // a 300-unit gap, then 200 of runway to land on... L2_RAMP2_I = L2_PIECES.length; straight(L2_RAMP_L, false, true, true); // ...ending in a second ramp px += hx * L2_GAP; pz += hz * L2_GAP; s += L2_GAP; L2_LAND2_I = L2_PIECES.length; straight(L2_X + L2_GAPX - L2_CURVE_R - px, true, false); // another gap, and the final stretch, which ends where it turns off... arc(-1, L2_CLIMB_TURN, L2_CURVE_R, L2_CURVE_UP); // ...in a right turn, climbing a little, into the gap in the rocks... var cl = Math.hypot(L2_X + L2_PEAK_X - px, L2_PEAK_Z - pz), cm = (L2_PEAK_Y - curY - L2_RAMP_T * (L2_RAMP_L - 4)) / (cl - L2_RAMP_L - 7 + 4); // ...then up the rock face to the peak: a steady slope that eases on, straight(cl - L2_RAMP_L, false, false, false, { sl0: 0, sl1: cm, E: 14, b0: 0, blend: true }); // blending into the rock as it climbs... L2_CLIMB_I = L2_PIECES.length; L2_CLIMB_SL = cm; straight(L2_RAMP_L, false, true, true, { sl0: cm, sl1: L2_RAMP_T, E: 8, b0: cl - L2_RAMP_L, blend: true, keepY: true }); // ...and ending in a ramp at the peak var cg = (L2_CAVE_Z - pz) / hz; px += hx * cg; pz += hz * cg; s += cg; note(); curY = L2_CAVE_Y; // a canyon to jump, then an octagonal cut in the crag's wall with a level floor L2_CAVE_I = L2_PIECES.length; straight(L2_CAVE_L, true, true); L2_NAR0 = L2_PIECES.length; // the wall at the cave's end is gone: the floor eases onto a 30 degree slope, runs down it and eases out at ground level var nHold = (L2_CAVE_Y - L2_Y - L2_RAMP_T * 12) / L2_RAMP_T; straight(12 + nHold, true, false, false, { sl0: 0, sl1: -L2_RAMP_T, E: 12, b0: 0, flat: true }); straight(12, false, false, false, { sl0: -L2_RAMP_T, sl1: 0, E: 12, b0: 0, flat: true }); L2_FLAT0 = s; for (var ni = 0; ni < L2_NSEGS.length; ni++) { var nl = L2_NSEGS[ni][0], nd = L2_NSEGS[ni][1]; if (Math.abs(nd) < 0.002) straight(nl); else arc(nd > 0 ? 1 : -1, Math.abs(nd) * 180 / Math.PI, nl / Math.abs(nd), 0); } arc(-1, 118, 640, 60); // up and out: a wide right turn (640 is the tightest she can hold at top speed) that climbs from 40 to 100, leaving the crags' inner shore arc(-1, 123.059, 640, 0); // level at 100, the loop round the bay to head east L2_BAYEND_I = L2_PIECES.length; straight(478 + 250 - 51); // back in at the shore at about Z -500, and 200 on east into the rock, to a sheer drop onto the Sink (its end, at X 1436) for (var nj = L2_NAR0; nj < L2_PIECES.length; nj++) L2_PIECES[nj].nar = true; })(); // ---- The Crags: a 500-wide range of brown rock spikes. Its centre line starts 200 east and 200 north of the caution sign, runs south across the final runway, bends west // in a quarter circle that meets the start's z line 1,000 east of the start, and carries on 200 west. Its shore slopes down into the water on every side. ---- var MT_HW = 500, MT_SKIRT = 612, MT_BASE = L2_Y - 5, MT_TOP = WATER_Y + 300, MT_S = 0, MT_LC = 500, l2Kind = 1; var MT_P0X = 0, MT_P0Z = 0, MT_R = 0, MT_ZA = 0, MT_LA = 0, MT_LTOT = 0; (function () { var lp = L2_PIECES[L2_LAND2_I], endX = L2_X + 1000; MT_P0X = lp.x0 + 120 + 200; MT_P0Z = lp.z0 - 6 - 200; // 200 east and 200 north of the sign MT_ZA = -(MT_P0X - endX); MT_R = MT_P0X - L2_X; MT_LA = MT_ZA - MT_P0Z; MT_LTOT = MT_LA + MT_R * Math.PI / 2 + MT_LC; // straight south, one smooth quarter turn west (from the same spot) that is heading west at X 0, Z 1000, then 500 more west L2_BOX[0] = Math.min(L2_BOX[0], L2_X - MT_LC - MT_SKIRT - 20); L2_BOX[1] = Math.max(L2_BOX[1], MT_P0X + MT_SKIRT + 20); L2_BOX[2] = Math.min(L2_BOX[2], MT_P0Z - 140); L2_BOX[3] = Math.max(L2_BOX[3], MT_ZA + MT_R + MT_SKIRT + 20); })(); // The Tunnel: a block of rock across the runway, 100 west of the range's centre line, with an octagonal opening 30 across (flat top, 8.3 per side) whose top is only 2 above // the runway. Only the top 2 of the octagon clear the floor, so a standing Molly (3.6 tall) meets a wall, and a sliding one (1 tall) passes under it. var GATE_X0 = MT_P0X - 90, GATE_Z = L2_PIECES[L2_LAND2_I].z0, GATE_L = 90, GATE_W = 60, GATE_TOP = L2_Y + 85, GATE_OCT = 30 / (1 + Math.SQRT2), gateIn = false; var OCV = L2_PIECES[L2_CAVE_I], OCV_W = 60, OCV_OA = 15, OCV_OC = GATE_OCT / Math.SQRT2, OCV_TOP = L2_CAVE_Y + 85; // the cave's octagon: 30 across, flat top and bottom, its floor level with the runway inside function ocvW(h) { return h < 0 || h > 30 ? -1 : h < OCV_OC ? OCV_OA - OCV_OC + h : h <= 30 - OCV_OC ? OCV_OA : OCV_OA - (h - (30 - OCV_OC)); } // half width of the opening h above its floor // Sideways distance to the range's centre line (the shore starts at MT_HW); sets MT_S, how far along it. Past either end the shore starts at once. function mtDist(x, z) { var best = 1e9, d, t = z - MT_P0Z, zc = MT_ZA + MT_R, xe = MT_P0X - MT_R; if (t >= 0 && t <= MT_LA) { best = Math.abs(x - MT_P0X); MT_S = t; } else if (t < 0 && t > -150) { best = Math.max(Math.abs(x - MT_P0X), MT_HW) - t; MT_S = 0; } var dx = x - xe, dz = z - MT_ZA; // the quarter turn, west if (dx >= 0 && dz >= 0) { d = Math.abs(Math.hypot(dx, dz) - MT_R); if (d < best) { best = d; MT_S = MT_LA + MT_R * Math.atan2(dz, dx); } } var tc = xe - x; // the last stretch, west if (tc >= 0 && tc <= MT_LC) { d = Math.abs(z - zc); if (d < best) { best = d; MT_S = MT_LA + MT_R * Math.PI / 2 + tc; } } else if (tc > MT_LC && tc < MT_LC + 150) { d = Math.max(Math.abs(z - zc), MT_HW) + (tc - MT_LC); if (d < best) { best = d; MT_S = MT_LTOT; } } return best; } // Rock spikes: one cone to every 64-unit cell, each with its own height (25 to 295), base width (about 56 to 184), slope profile and lopsided outline. function mtSpikes(x, z) { var CS = 64, ci = Math.floor(x / CS), cj = Math.floor(z / CS), best = 0; for (var di = -2; di <= 2; di++) for (var dj = -2; dj <= 2; dj++) { var a = ci + di, b = cj + dj, r1 = rand01(a, b, 31), r2 = rand01(a, b, 32), r3 = rand01(a, b, 33), r4 = rand01(a, b, 34), r5 = rand01(a, b, 35); var px = (a + 0.15 + 0.7 * r1) * CS, pz = (b + 0.15 + 0.7 * r2) * CS, dxp = x - px, dzp = z - pz; var rad = 28 + 64 * r3, ang = Math.atan2(dzp, dxp), wob = 1 + 0.2 * Math.sin(3 * ang + r1 * 6.28) + 0.1 * Math.sin(5 * ang + r2 * 6.28); var q = 1 - Math.hypot(dxp, dzp) / (rad * wob); if (q > 0) { var v = (25 + 270 * Math.pow(r4, 2.2)) * Math.pow(q, 0.65 + 1.1 * r5); if (v > best) best = v; } } return best; } // The two gaps the range now reaches stay open water: a channel through the rock along each, so the jump is as threatening as before. var MT_GAPS = [[L2_RAMP2_I, L2_LAND2_I]].map(function (g) { var r = L2_PIECES[g[0]], l = L2_PIECES[g[1]]; return [r.x0 + r.hx * r.len, r.z0 + r.hz * r.len, l.x0, l.z0]; }); // The jump to the cave: no channel, the crags fill it, but nothing rises into the arc of Segs. The rock is kept below that arc along the line from the ramp's lip to the cave's mouth. var AIR = (function () { var r = L2_PIECES[L2_CLIMB_I], c = L2_PIECES[L2_CAVE_I], x0 = r.x0 + r.hx * r.len, z0 = r.z0 + r.hz * r.len; var yTop = r.y0 + l2RiseG(r.len, r.lf.sl0, r.lf.sl1, r.lf.E), yEnd = L2_CAVE_Y + 14; return { x0: x0, z0: z0, dx: c.x0 - x0, dz: c.z0 - z0, D: Math.hypot(c.x0 - x0, c.z0 - z0), yTop: yTop, yEnd: yEnd }; })(); function airY(t) { return AIR.yTop + 3 + (AIR.yEnd - AIR.yTop - 3) * t + 4 * 33 * t * (1 - t); } // the Segs' height a fraction t of the way across function airCap(x, z) { // how high the rock may rise here: well below the arc, and below the cave's floor in front of its mouth var dd = AIR.dx * AIR.dx + AIR.dz * AIR.dz, t = ((x - AIR.x0) * AIR.dx + (z - AIR.z0) * AIR.dz) / dd, tc = t < 0 ? 0 : t > 1 ? 1 : t; var d = Math.hypot(x - AIR.x0 - AIR.dx * tc, z - AIR.z0 - AIR.dz * tc); if (d > 70) return 1e9; var cap = Math.min(airY(tc) - 14, L2_CAVE_Y - 6 + 0.5 * (1 - tc) * AIR.D); return cap + 500 * smooth01((d - 26) / 44); } function mtGapDist(x, z) { var best = 1e9; for (var i = 0; i < MT_GAPS.length; i++) { var g = MT_GAPS[i], dx = g[2] - g[0], dz = g[3] - g[1], t = ((x - g[0]) * dx + (z - g[1]) * dz) / (dx * dx + dz * dz); t = t < 0 ? 0 : t > 1 ? 1 : t; var d = Math.hypot(x - g[0] - dx * t, z - g[1] - dz * t); if (d < best) best = d; } return best; } // ---- The Sink: an irregular ovoid of low, poisoned ground set into the crags between Z -500 and Z -1500 (about 750 across). Its rim was fitted (OV_SC: the rim's // distance from the centre at 48 angles, as a fraction of the ovoid's own outline) to run through the gaps between the crags that are already there. The ground is rolling // hills with small crags scattered over it, each with ground all round it; everything outside the crags is toxic. ---- var OV_CX = L2_X + 1672, OV_CZ = -1000, OV_A = 175, OV_B = 500, OV_N = 3.2, OV_Y = 50, OV_SC = [1.008, 0.991, 1.018, 1.024, 0.996, 0.975, 0.975, 0.985, 0.985, 0.974, 0.964, 0.953, 0.939, 0.926, 0.912, 0.915, 0.958, 1.014, 1.028, 1.000, 0.990, 1.009, 1.015, 1.015, 1.035, 1.044, 1.018, 1.003, 1.034, 1.066, 1.055, 1.016, 0.984, 0.980, 1.002, 1.022, 1.031, 1.046, 1.064, 1.078, 1.084, 1.049, 0.988, 0.959, 0.955, 0.964, 1.005, 1.034], ovTox = 0, ovCrag = 0, ovK = 0; function ovKAt(x, z) { // 1 well inside the ovoid, 0 outside it, a blend across the rim var kc = 0, cxr = x - L2_X, zc = Math.abs(z + 500.7); // the mouth: a short, wide cut through the crags from the path's end into the ovoid if (cxr > 1436 && cxr < 1680 && zc < 100) kc = smooth01((cxr - 1436) / 14) * (1 - smooth01((cxr - 1600) / 70)) * (1 - smooth01((zc - 30 - (cxr - 1436) * 0.12) / 40)); var dx = (x - OV_CX) / OV_A, dz = (z - OV_CZ) / OV_B; if (dx > 1.5 || dx < -1.5 || dz > 1.5 || dz < -1.5) return kc; var r = Math.hypot(dx, dz); if (r < 1e-6) return 1; var c = dx / r, sn = dz / r, r0 = Math.pow(Math.pow(Math.abs(c), OV_N) + Math.pow(Math.abs(sn), OV_N), -1 / OV_N); var f = Math.atan2(dz, dx) / (2 * Math.PI); if (f < 0) f += 1; f *= 48; var i0 = Math.floor(f) % 48, fr = f - Math.floor(f); var sc = OV_SC[i0] * (1 - fr) + OV_SC[(i0 + 1) % 48] * fr, q = r / (r0 * sc); var ke = 1 - smooth01((q - 0.85) / 0.3); if (z > -1100) ke *= smooth01(((1822 + L2_X - x) - 0.553 * (z + 461)) * 0.875 / 50 - 1.4); // keep well clear of the rock path that runs down the east side return Math.max(kc, ke); } var L2_BARE = true; // Oceana Crags without its built-in path: no lane, runways, ramps, pads, gap channels, narrow walled path or bridge. Just the crags, the Sink and the sea, for paths and objects laid in the Path Editor var OV_FEATURES = false; // the Sink's maze of ledges, its scatter of crags and the Big Minion's pit are all taken out, to be placed by hand in the Path Editor function ovLedge(x, z) { if (!OV_FEATURES) return 0; // the Sink's maze: walls 7.5 thick on a 48-unit grid, each 2 or 5 tall with square faces (they use the game's ledge cells). A binary-tree maze: every cell opens east or south, and keeps the other wall if (z > -660 || ovKAt(x, z) < 0.9) return 0; // none at the mouth, so she lands on open ground var MC = 48, HW = 3.75, u = x - (OV_CX - 5 * MC), v = z - (OV_CZ - 9 * MC), ui = Math.round(u / MC), vi = Math.round(v / MC), h = 0, k, c, w; if (Math.abs(u - ui * MC) <= HW) { // a wall between cell ui-1 and cell ui, across the row(s) at this height (it runs on half a wall's width past each end to close the corners) var j0 = Math.floor(v / MC), fv = v - j0 * MC, js = [j0]; if (fv < HW) js.push(j0 - 1); else if (fv > MC - HW) js.push(j0 + 1); for (k = 0; k < js.length; k++) if (rand01(ui - 1, js[k], 91) < 0.5) { w = rand01(ui - 1, js[k], 92) < 0.5 ? 2 : 5; if (w > h) h = w; } } if (Math.abs(v - vi * MC) <= HW) { var i0 = Math.floor(u / MC), fu = u - i0 * MC, is = [i0]; if (fu < HW) is.push(i0 - 1); else if (fu > MC - HW) is.push(i0 + 1); for (k = 0; k < is.length; k++) if (rand01(is[k], vi - 1, 91) >= 0.5) { w = rand01(is[k], vi - 1, 93) < 0.5 ? 2 : 5; if (w > h) h = w; } } return h; } function ovGround(x, z) { // hills, plus a scatter of small crags (one cone to a 60-unit cell, most cells, none closer than 1 apart) var h = OV_Y + 6 * Math.sin(x * 0.019 + 1.3) * Math.cos(z * 0.016 + 0.4) + 4.5 * Math.sin(x * 0.041 + z * 0.033 + 2.1) + 3 * Math.sin(z * 0.058 - x * 0.027 + 0.7) + 2 * Math.sin(x * 0.11 + z * 0.09); var CS = 60, ci = Math.floor(x / CS), cj = Math.floor(z / CS), best = 0; for (var di = -1; di <= 1; di++) for (var dj = -1; dj <= 1; dj++) { var a = ci + di, b = cj + dj; if (!OV_FEATURES || rand01(a, b, 71) > 0.85) continue; var px = (a + 0.2 + 0.6 * rand01(a, b, 72)) * CS, pz = (b + 0.2 + 0.6 * rand01(a, b, 73)) * CS, dxp = x - px, dzp = z - pz; var rad = 5 + 5 * rand01(a, b, 74), ang = Math.atan2(dzp, dxp), wob = 1 + 0.1 * Math.sin(3 * ang + rand01(a, b, 75) * 6.28) + 0.05 * Math.sin(5 * ang + rand01(a, b, 76) * 6.28); var q = 1 - Math.hypot(dxp, dzp) / (rad * wob); if (q > 0) { var v = (12 + 40 * rand01(a, b, 77)) * Math.pow(q, 0.7 + 0.7 * rand01(a, b, 78)); if (v > best) best = v; } } ovCrag = best; return h + best; } function mountainH(x, z, dRun) { var h = mountainH0(x, z, dRun); ovTox = 0; ovK = 0; var k = ovKAt(x, z); if (k <= 0) return h; ovK = k; var g = ovGround(x, z); ovTox = k > 0.5 && ovCrag < 4 ? 1 : 0; return h + (g - h) * k; } function mountainH0(x, z, dRun) { var lat = mtDist(x, z), dGap = L2_BARE ? 1e9 : mtGapDist(x, z); if (dGap < 70) dRun = Math.min(dRun, dGap); if (dGap < 45) { var hc = (lat >= MT_HW ? MT_BASE - (lat - MT_HW) * 1.2 : MT_BASE) - (45 - dGap) * 1.2; return lat > MT_SKIRT ? L2_VOID : hc; } // a channel, sloping down to below the water if (lat > MT_SKIRT) return L2_VOID; if (lat >= MT_HW) return MT_BASE - (lat - MT_HW) * 1.2; // the shore: down into the water var env = (1 - smooth01((lat - (MT_HW - 50)) / 50)) * smooth01(MT_S / 150) * smooth01((MT_LTOT - MT_S) / 150) * smooth01((dRun - 14) / 56); var sp = mtSpikes(x, z); var y = MT_BASE + sp * env + (rand01(Math.round(x / GRID), Math.round(z / GRID), 36) - 0.5) * 6 * Math.min(1, sp * env / 40); if (y > MT_BASE && !L2_BARE) { var ac = airCap(x, z); if (y > ac) y = Math.max(MT_BASE, ac); } return y > MT_TOP ? MT_TOP : y; } // Distance from (x, z) to the centre line (also sets L2_S, how far along it, L2_PS, how far along its own piece, and L2_PI, which piece). function l2Dist(x, z, loose) { // loose: ignore the square ends (used to decide which tiles to build, so a tile holding only a runway's end is not skipped) var best = 1e9, n = L2_PIECES.length; for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) { var p = L2_PIECES[i], d, ps; if (p.t === 's') { var dx = x - p.x0, dz = z - p.z0, t = dx * p.hx + dz * p.hz, perp = dx * p.hz - dz * p.hx; if (!loose && ((p.sqEnd && t > p.len) || (p.sqStart && t < 0))) continue; // square ends: the start pad, the top of the ramp and both sides of the gap var tc = t < 0 ? 0 : t > p.len ? p.len : t; d = Math.hypot(t - tc, perp); ps = tc; } else { var vx = x - p.cx, vz = z - p.cz, c = p.ux * vz - p.uz * vx, dt = p.ux * vx + p.uz * vz, ang = p.sig * Math.atan2(-c, dt); if (ang < 0 || ang > p.th) continue; d = Math.abs(Math.hypot(vx, vz) - p.R); ps = p.R * ang; } if (d < best) { best = d; L2_S = p.s0 + ps; L2_PS = ps; L2_PI = i; } } return best; } function l2RiseG(s, sl0, sl1, E) { // height gained s along a stretch whose slope eases linearly from sl0 to sl1 over E, then holds if (s <= 0) return 0; return s < E ? sl0 * s + (sl1 - sl0) * s * s / (2 * E) : sl0 * E + (sl1 - sl0) * E / 2 + sl1 * (s - E); } function l2Rise(s) { // height up the ramp: eases onto 30 degrees over the first few units if (s <= 0) return 0; return s < L2_EASE ? L2_RAMP_T * s * s / (2 * L2_EASE) : L2_RAMP_T * (s - L2_EASE / 2); } function narrowH(x, z, dr, pc) { // the path out of the cave: a flat lane 6 wide, and a wall of rock on each side that shoots up from its edge var hp = narY(pc, L2_PS), hw = narHW(x, z); if (dr <= hw) { var nl = mountainH(x, z, 1000), wv = smooth01(ovK / 0.35); l2Kind = ovK > 0.3 ? 2 : 1; return (nl + (hp - nl) * smooth01((nl - 25) / 20)) * (1 - wv) + nl * wv; } // (over the Sink the mesh stays ground, and the slab carries the path) // over the water the mesh keeps no column here: the floating slab is its own mesh var d = dr - hw, k = L2_S / 45, ki = Math.floor(k), kf = k - ki; kf = kf * kf * (3 - 2 * kf); var wmax = 22 + 26 * (rand01(ki, 5, 57) * (1 - kf) + rand01(ki + 1, 5, 57) * kf); // how high the wall stands, changing slowly along the path var dTop = wmax / 3, w = d <= dTop ? 3 * d : Math.max(0, wmax - 0.9 * (d - dTop)); w += (rand01(Math.round(x / GRID), Math.round(z / GRID), 58) - 0.5) * 5 * Math.min(1, d / 5); var nat = mountainH(x, z, 1000), y = hp + w; if (nat > y) y += (nat - y) * smooth01((d - 5) / 9); // the natural crags only take over a little way back, so the wall's face is a clean slope up from the lane y = nat + (y - nat) * smooth01((nat - 25) / 20); // no walls where the path leaves the crags: out over the water it is a bare causeway y = y + (nat - y) * smooth01(ovK / 0.5); // no walls over the Sink if (d > 30) { var ft = smooth01((d - 30) / 30); y = y * (1 - ft) + nat * ft; } // and well away from the path the ground is simply the crags again (no plateau at path height) return y; } function level2H(x, z) { l2Kind = 2; ovTox = 0; ovK = 0; if (x < L2_BOX[0] || x > L2_BOX[1] || z < L2_BOX[2] || z > L2_BOX[3]) return L2_VOID; if (L2_BARE) return mountainH(x, z, 1000); // no path: the crags reach everywhere they would away from it var dr = l2Dist(x, z), pc = L2_PIECES[L2_PI]; if (pc.nar) return narrowH(x, z, dr, pc); if (pc.blend) { // the climb: a lane whose edges melt into the rock, and which turns from stone to rock itself var lf = pc.lf, sp = pc.b0 + L2_PS, hp = pc.y0 + l2RiseG(L2_PS, lf.sl0, lf.sl1, lf.E); if (dr <= L2_HW) { l2Kind = sp < 40 ? 1 : 2; return hp; } var u = Math.min(1, sp / 100), nat = mountainH(x, z, dr + 400 * smooth01(sp / 50)), f = smooth01((dr - L2_HW) / (3 + 15 * smooth01(u))); return hp + (nat - hp) * f; } if (dr > L2_HW) return mountainH(x, z, dr); l2Kind = 1; return pc.ramp ? pc.y0 + l2RiseG(L2_PS, pc.lf ? pc.lf.sl0 : 0, pc.lf ? pc.lf.sl1 : L2_RAMP_T, pc.lf ? pc.lf.E : L2_EASE) : pc.dy ? pc.y0 + pc.dy * smooth01(L2_PS / pc.len) : pc.y0; } // ---- Level 3, the Sandbox: flat patchy grass at x = 60000 and up, shaped by whatever the Path Editor made (its exported file). The layout is kept in editor // coordinates (the path starts at 0, 0 heading north) and shifted by L3_X into the world. ---- var L3_X = 60000, L3_MIN = 50000; var SB = null, sbNoRamps = false; // sbNoRamps: while each ramp's foot is measured, ramps are left out of the ground function sbHash(c) { return { c: c, m: {} }; } var TOX_C = 32; function sbToxAt(B, ex, z) { return B.toxH ? B.toxH.m[(Math.floor(ex / TOX_C) + 4096) * 8192 + Math.floor(z / TOX_C) + 4096] : null; } // the toxic patches whose reach covers this point's cell (or null) function sbIn(e, x, z, g) { // is (x, z) inside this footprint entry, grown by g? Rounded pieces are discs along the line; squared pieces are short oriented boxes (e.hl along the line, e.half across) if (!e.sq) return Math.hypot(e.x - x, e.z - z) <= e.half + g; var dx = x - e.x, dz = z - e.z, c = Math.cos(e.th), s = Math.sin(e.th); return Math.abs(dx * c + dz * s) <= e.half + g && Math.abs(dx * s - dz * c) <= e.hl + g; } function sbAdd(h, x, z, v) { var k = (Math.floor(x / h.c) + 4096) * 8192 + Math.floor(z / h.c) + 4096; (h.m[k] || (h.m[k] = [])).push(v); } function sbNear(h, x, z, rad, fn) { var i0 = Math.floor((x - rad) / h.c), i1 = Math.floor((x + rad) / h.c), j0 = Math.floor((z - rad) / h.c), j1 = Math.floor((z + rad) / h.c); for (var i = i0; i <= i1; i++) for (var j = j0; j <= j1; j++) { var a = h.m[(i + 4096) * 8192 + j + 4096]; if (a) for (var q = 0; q < a.length; q++) fn(a[q]); } } function cragLayout(c) { // a patch of crags: where each stands and how big, from the patch's seed (the same seed always gives the same crags) var s = (c.seed >>> 0) || 1, out = [], i, t, j, R = c.r, n = Math.max(1, Math.min(500, Math.round(c.count))); function rnd() { s = (s + 0x6D2B79F5) >>> 0; var u = s; u = Math.imul(u ^ u >>> 15, u | 1); u ^= u + Math.imul(u ^ u >>> 7, u | 61); return ((u ^ u >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296; } function vary(a, v, lo, hi) { return Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, a + (rnd() * 2 - 1) * v)); } for (i = 0; i < n; i++) { var rad = vary(c.base, c.baseV, 1, 15), h = vary(c.hgt, c.hgtV, 0.3, 200), want = vary(c.gap, c.gapV, 0, 1000), x = 0, z = 0; for (t = 0; t < 40; t++) { // the gap asked for eases to nothing over the tries, so a crowded patch squeezes its crags closer rather than dropping any var f = 1 - t / 39, a = rnd() * 6.2832, d = Math.sqrt(rnd()) * R, ok = true; x = Math.cos(a) * d; z = Math.sin(a) * d; for (j = 0; j < out.length; j++) { var q = out[j]; if (Math.hypot(q.x - x, q.z - z) < q.rad + rad + want * f) { ok = false; break; } } if (ok) break; } out.push({ x: x, z: z, rad: rad, h: h }); } return out; } function sbSeg(p) { // a path segment as the game builds it. Old files have straights and left/right arcs; the same thing is a segment with no curve angle, or with a negative one var o = { dy: p.dy || 0, roll: p.roll || 0, b0: p.b0, w: p.w > 0 ? p.w : 0, w0: p.w0 > 0 ? p.w0 : 0, wm: p.wm, ov: p.ov ? 1 : 0, gf: p.gf ? 1 : 0, tx: p.tx > 0 ? Math.min(3, p.tx | 0) : 0, fl: p.fl > 0 ? p.fl : 0, sx: p.sx, sz: p.sz, sy: p.sy, sh: p.sh, sm: p.sm ? 1 : 0, smL: p.smL > 0 ? p.smL : 20 }; if (p.disc) { o.disc = 1; o.R = Math.max(2, p.rad > 0 ? p.rad : 40); o.hd = p.h; o.cs = p.cs || 0; o.bk = p.bk || 0; o.ang = 0; o.len = 2 * o.R; o.dy = 0; o.roll = 0; o.w = 0; o.gf = 0; return o; } // a circular deck: a radius instead of a length; the next segment leaves from its rim, where its heading points if (p.t === 'straight') { o.ang = 0; o.len = Math.max(0, p.len || 0); } else if (p.t === 'arc') { var a = Math.abs(p.ang || 0), r = Math.max(1, p.r || 1); o.ang = (p.dir === 'R' ? 1 : -1) * a; o.len = r * a * Math.PI / 180; } else { o.ang = p.ang || 0; o.len = Math.max(0, p.len === undefined ? 200 : p.len); } return o; } function sbTerrBase(kind, ex, z, top) { // top: the surface a path rides on, which over water is the water's surface rather than the ground far below it // the level's own ground at a point, before any path is laid over it (a path that follows the ground, or a slab being compared with it, starts from this) if (kind === 'level2') { var h2 = worldH0(L2_X + ex, z + L2_SBZ); return top ? Math.max(h2, WATER_Y) : h2; } if (kind === 'level1') return worldH0(ex, z); if (kind === 'grass') return terrainFlat(ex, z); if (kind === 'crags') return Math.min(300, mtSpikes(ex, z)); if (kind === 'ocean') return top ? -10 : L2_VOID; return 0; // flat ground; the Giant Hill measures a path from its own surface, so that is 0 too } function sbDiscY(D, x, z) { // the height of a circular deck at (x, z) (Sandbox coordinates): its slope rises (+) or falls (-) toward the centre, and its bank tips it to the right (+) of its heading, as a path's bank does var dx = x - D.cx, dz = z - D.cz, r = Math.hypot(dx, dz); return D.base + (D.R - Math.min(D.R, r)) * D.tanC - (dx * Math.cos(D.hh) + dz * Math.sin(D.hh)) * D.tanB; } function sbVcurves(sm, VJ) { // gradual slope changes, the same as the Path Editor's vcurves: at a joint where either segment asks for it, the grade eases from one to the other over the blend length (half each side), as a parabola that meets both straight grades for (var q = 1; q < VJ.length; q++) { var A = VJ[q - 1], B = VJ[q]; if (!B.cont || A.gf || B.gf || !(A.sm || B.sm)) continue; var L = Math.min(Math.max(A.sm ? A.smL : 0, B.sm ? B.smL : 0), A.len, B.len), g1 = A.g, g2 = B.g; if (!(L > 0.01) || Math.abs(g2 - g1) < 1e-9) continue; var h = L / 2, sJ = sm[B.i0].s; for (var k = A.i0; k <= B.i1; k++) { var d = sm[k].s - sJ; if (d <= -h || d >= h) continue; sm[k].y += g1 * d + (g2 - g1) * (d + h) * (d + h) / (2 * L) - (d < 0 ? g1 : g2) * d; if (sm[k].fr) sm[k].fl = sm[k].y - sm[k].fr > sm[k].tb ? sm[k].fr : 0; // a slab's underside: floating or merged, from the new height } } } function sbBuild(j) { j = j || {}; var B = { discs: [], width: j.width || 9, terrain: j.terrain || '', samples: [], total: 0, objs: [], trees: [], minions: [], gazers: [], toxic: [], segs: [], tips: [], goals: [], spawners: [], strips: [], meshes: [], tipDone: {}, ramps: [], hills: [], lakes: [], bigs: [], caves: [], shafts: [], patches: [], timeLimit: Math.max(0, +j.timeLimit || 0), ranks: null, noCave: false, caveOpen: null, caveCov: null, caveR: 8, cps: [], signs: [], movers: [], plates: [], targets: [], trig: {}, tgBits: [], T: 0, signDone: {}, waterY: null }; var sm = B.samples, x = 0, z = 0, th = 0, s = 0, y0b = (j.terrain === 'level2' ? L2_Y : 0), y = y0b, STEP = 4; // Oceana Crags' path runs at 40 var PATHS = [{ x: 0, z: 0, th: 0, y: 0, pieces: j.pieces || [] }].concat((j.extraPaths || []).map(function (e) { return { x: e.x || 0, z: e.z || 0, th: (e.heading || 0) * Math.PI / 180, y: e.rise || 0, pieces: e.pieces || [] }; })); B.pathStart = []; B.pathEnd = []; PATHS.forEach(function (pth, pk) { // each path is its own chain; its distances along it count from pk * 100000, so a stamp's "along" says which path it is on x = pth.x; z = pth.z; th = pth.th; s = pk * 100000; y = y0b + pth.y; var rl = 0, wd = B.width, sg0 = pth.pieces.length ? sbSeg(pth.pieces[0]) : null; if (sg0) { if (sg0.sx !== undefined) x = sg0.sx; if (sg0.sz !== undefined) z = sg0.sz; if (sg0.sy !== undefined) y = y0b + sg0.sy; if (sg0.sh !== undefined) th = sg0.sh * Math.PI / 180; if (sg0.b0 !== undefined) rl = sg0.b0; if (sg0.w0 > 0) wd = sg0.w0; } sm.push({ x: x, z: z, th: th, s: s, y: y, roll: rl, w: wd, brk: pk > 0 }); B.pathStart.push(s); var VJ = []; // the joints between this path's segments, for gradual slope changes (vcurves) pth.pieces.forEach(function (p0, pi) { var px0 = x, pz0 = z, py0 = y; var p = sbSeg(p0), i, n, rl0, rl1 = p.roll, w0, w1, wstep = p.wm === 'step', pov = p.ov, gf = p.gf, d, y0, tb, pfl, L2 = 0; // fl: a slab this thick, floating where its underside is clear of the ground and merged with the ground where it is not. Width: each segment may end at its own width, tapering along it or stepping at its start if (pi > 0 && (p.sx !== undefined || p.sz !== undefined || p.sy !== undefined || p.sh !== undefined || p.b0 !== undefined || p.w0 > 0)) { // a start set by hand: it does not pick up where the last one ended var nx = p.sx !== undefined ? p.sx : x, nz = p.sz !== undefined ? p.sz : z, jump = Math.hypot(nx - x, nz - z) > 0.5; x = nx; z = nz; if (p.sy !== undefined) y = y0b + p.sy; if (p.sh !== undefined) th = p.sh * Math.PI / 180; if (p.b0 !== undefined) rl = p.b0; if (p.w0 > 0) wd = p.w0; sm.push({ x: x, z: z, th: th, s: s, y: y, roll: rl, w: wd, brk: jump, tx: p.tx }); } rl0 = rl; w0 = wd; w1 = p.w > 0 ? p.w : wd; y0 = y; var vj = { i0: sm.length - 1, len: p.len, g: p.len > 0 ? p.dy / p.len : 0, sm: !!p.sm, smL: p.smL, gf: !!gf, cont: pi > 0 && Math.hypot(x - px0, z - pz0) < 0.5 && Math.abs(y - py0) < 0.01 }; function put(i2, n2, yy) { var tb2 = 0, dkG = j.terrain === 'level2'; if (p.fl && !dkG) tb2 = sbTerrBase(j.terrain, x, z); if (gf) yy = sbTerrBase(j.terrain, x, z, true) + p.dy; y = yy; sm.push({ fr: p.fl && !dkG ? p.fl : 0, tb: tb2, x: x, z: z, th: th, s: s, y: y, roll: rl0 + (rl1 - rl0) * smooth01(i2 / n2), w: wstep ? w1 : w0 + (w1 - w0) * smooth01(i2 / n2), fl: p.fl && !dkG && y - p.fl > tb2 ? p.fl : 0, dk: dkG && p.fl > 0 ? p.fl : 0, pv: pov, tx: p.tx }); } // (on Oceana the path is a deck of its own everywhere, and a slab thickness is simply how deep that deck is: dk) if (p.disc) { // a circular deck: she comes in at its rim; its centre is its radius straight ahead; the next segment leaves from the rim where the deck's heading points (the same heading as the way in: straight across) var Rd = p.R, hh = p.hd !== undefined && p.hd !== null ? p.hd * Math.PI / 180 : th, dcx = x + Math.sin(th) * Rd, dcz = z - Math.cos(th) * Rd; var D = { cx: dcx, cz: dcz, R: Rd, hh: hh, tanC: Math.tan(Math.max(-89, Math.min(89, p.cs)) * Math.PI / 180), tanB: Math.tan(Math.max(-89, Math.min(89, p.bk)) * Math.PI / 180), base: 0, ov: pov, tx: p.tx || 0, dk: p.fl || 0, idx: B.discs.length }; D.base = y - (sbDiscY(D, x, z) - D.base); B.discs.push(D); // the rim where she comes in is at the height the last segment ended at var ex0 = x, ez0 = z, nl = Math.max(1, Math.round(Rd / STEP)), dsk = D.idx + 1; for (i = 1; i <= nl; i++) { x = ex0 + (dcx - ex0) * i / nl; z = ez0 + (dcz - ez0) * i / nl; s += Rd / nl; y = sbDiscY(D, x, z); sm.push({ x: x, z: z, th: th, s: s, y: y, roll: 0, w: wd, dsk: dsk, tx: p.tx }); } var ox1 = dcx + Math.sin(hh) * Rd, oz1 = dcz - Math.cos(hh) * Rd; th = hh; sm.push({ x: x, z: z, th: th, s: s, y: y, roll: 0, w: wd, dsk: dsk, tx: p.tx }); // at the centre it turns to the way out (two samples at one spot) for (i = 1; i <= nl; i++) { x = dcx + (ox1 - dcx) * i / nl; z = dcz + (oz1 - dcz) * i / nl; s += Rd / nl; y = sbDiscY(D, x, z); sm.push({ x: x, z: z, th: th, s: s, y: y, roll: 0, w: wd, dsk: dsk, tx: p.tx }); } vj.gf = true; w1 = wd; } else if (Math.abs(p.ang) < 1e-9) { n = Math.max(1, Math.round(p.len / STEP)); d = p.len / n; for (i = 1; i <= n; i++) { x += Math.sin(th) * d; z -= Math.cos(th) * d; s += d; put(i, n, y0 + p.dy * i / n); } } else { var A = Math.abs(p.ang) * Math.PI / 180, R = Math.max(1e-6, p.len / A), sg = p.ang > 0 ? 1 : -1, L = p.len, th0 = th, cx = x + Math.cos(th0) * R * sg, cz = z + Math.sin(th0) * R * sg; n = Math.max(1, Math.round(L / STEP)); var s0 = s; for (i = 1; i <= n; i++) { var a = A * i / n, t2 = th0 + sg * a; x = cx - Math.cos(t2) * R * sg; z = cz - Math.sin(t2) * R * sg; th = t2; s = s0 + L * i / n; put(i, n, y0 + p.dy * i / n); } } wd = w1; rl = rl1; vj.i1 = sm.length - 1; VJ.push(vj); }); sbVcurves(sm, VJ, j.terrain); B.pathEnd.push(sm[sm.length - 1].s); }); B.total = sm[sm.length - 1].s; B.minY = 1e9; sm.forEach(function (q) { if (q.y < B.minY) B.minY = q.y; }); B.hw = B.width / 2; B.hwMax = B.hw; sm.forEach(function (q) { if (q.w / 2 > B.hwMax) B.hwMax = q.w / 2; q.wl = Math.abs(q.roll || 0) > 80.5; }); // wl: banked past 80 degrees, so a true wall (a tilted plane), not part of the height map if (j.ranks) { var rk = j.ranks; B.ranks = [[+rk.S || 800000, 'S', '#e8a400'], [+rk.A || 720000, 'A', '#2e9b4b'], [+rk.B || 640000, 'B', '#2b7fd0'], [+rk.C || 560000, 'C', '#8a5ad0'], [+rk.D || 480000, 'D', '#c9602b'], [0, 'E', '#7a6a5a']]; } function at(sv) { if (sm.length < 2) return { x: 0, z: 0, th: 0, y: 0 }; var pk0 = Math.max(0, Math.min(B.pathEnd.length - 1, Math.floor((sv + 0.5) / 100000))); sv = Math.max(B.pathStart[pk0], Math.min(B.pathEnd[pk0], sv)); var lo = 0, hi = sm.length - 2; while (lo < hi) { var mid = (lo + hi + 1) >> 1; if (sm[mid].s <= sv) lo = mid; else hi = mid - 1; } var i = lo; var a = sm[i], b = sm[i + 1], t = b.s > a.s ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (sv - a.s) / (b.s - a.s))) : 0; return { x: a.x + (b.x - a.x) * t, z: a.z + (b.z - a.z) * t, th: a.th + (b.th - a.th) * t, y: a.y + (b.y - a.y) * t }; } var byId = {}; function base(o, depth) { if (o.after !== undefined && depth < 60) { var t = byId[o.after]; if (t && t !== o) { var e = shapeAt(t, t.len, depth + 1); return { x: e.x, z: e.z, th: e.th + (o.turn || 0) * Math.PI / 180 }; } } return { x: o.x || 0, z: o.z || 0, th: (o.th || 0) * Math.PI / 180 }; } function shapeAt(o, d, depth) { depth = depth || 0; if (o.follow !== false && o.after === undefined) { var p = at(o.s + d); return { x: p.x + Math.cos(p.th) * o.u, z: p.z + Math.sin(p.th) * o.u, th: p.th }; } var b0 = base(o, depth), h0 = b0.th, Bn = (o.bend || 0) * Math.PI / 180, L = Math.max(1, o.len), dd = Math.min(L, d); if (Math.abs(Bn) < 1e-4) return { x: b0.x + Math.sin(h0) * dd, z: b0.z - Math.cos(h0) * dd, th: h0 }; var Rr = L / Bn, t2 = h0 + Bn * dd / L; return { x: b0.x + Math.cos(h0) * Rr - Math.cos(t2) * Rr, z: b0.z + Math.sin(h0) * Rr - Math.sin(t2) * Rr, th: t2 }; } var STRIPT = { segchain: 1, ledge: 1, platform: 1, gap: 1, mud: 1 }; function pos(o) { var r = (o.rot || 0) * Math.PI / 180; if (STRIPT[o.t]) { var q = shapeAt(o, 0); return { x: q.x, z: q.z, th: q.th }; } if (o.s !== undefined) { var p = at(o.s); return { x: p.x + Math.cos(p.th) * o.u, z: p.z + Math.sin(p.th) * o.u, th: p.th + r }; } return { x: o.x, z: o.z, th: r }; } // the objects (j.objects || []).forEach(function (r) { var o = { t: r.type, rot: r.rot || 0, id: r.id, y: r.y || 0, ind: r.heightMode === 'independent', ya: typeof r.yAbs === 'number' ?
