// floating pieces: their own slabs, found here B.ledgeH = ledgeH; B.gapH = gapH; B.mudH = mudH; B.coneH = coneH; B.pathH = pathH; B.maxL = maxL; B.maxG = maxG; B.maxM = maxM; var toxH = sbHash(TOX_C); B.toxic.forEach(function (t) { // toxic patches: each is filed in every cell its square reach covers, so a point looks in its own cell only (sbToxAt) t.cn = Math.cos(-t.th); t.sn = Math.sin(-t.th); for (var ta = Math.floor((t.x - t.rr) / TOX_C); ta <= Math.floor((t.x + t.rr) / TOX_C); ta++) for (var tb = Math.floor((t.z - t.rr) / TOX_C); tb <= Math.floor((t.z + t.rr) / TOX_C); tb++) sbAdd(toxH, (ta + 0.5) * TOX_C, (tb + 0.5) * TOX_C, t); }); B.toxH = toxH; B.hillsT = B.hills.filter(function (h) { return h.T !== undefined; }); B.dLedgeH = null; B.dHills = []; if (B.terrain === 'level2') { // Oceana: a ledge that starts on a path deck, or a hill centred on one, stands on the deck (raising its top, see sbDeckTop) instead of on the ground hidden under it var keepSB1 = SB, keepLH = B.ledgeH, keepHl = B.hills, keepHT = B.hillsT; SB = B; try { B.ledgeH = sbHash(ledgeH.c); B.hills = []; B.hillsT = []; var onDk = function (x, z) { var dk = sbDeckTop(L3_X + x, z, null); return dk > -1e8 && dk > level3H(L3_X + x, z) + 0.5; }; B.objs.forEach(function (o) { if (o.t === 'ledge') { var q0 = shapeAt(o, 0); o.dk = onDk(q0.x, q0.z); } }); keepHl.forEach(function (h) { h.dk = onDk(h.x, h.z); }); } finally { B.ledgeH = keepLH; B.hills = keepHl; B.hillsT = keepHT; SB = keepSB1; } var dLH = sbHash(ledgeH.c), anyDL = false; for (var lhk in ledgeH.m) ledgeH.m[lhk] = ledgeH.m[lhk].filter(function (e) { if (!e.o.dk) return true; (dLH.m[lhk] || (dLH.m[lhk] = [])).push(e); anyDL = true; return false; }); B.dLedgeH = anyDL ? dLH : null; B.dHills = B.hills.filter(function (h) { return h.dk; }); B.hills = B.hills.filter(function (h) { return !h.dk; }); B.hillsT = B.hills.filter(function (h) { return h.T !== undefined; }); B.dHillR = 0; B.dHills.forEach(function (h) { B.dHillR = Math.max(B.dHillR, h.r); }); } var lT = B.objs.filter(function (o) { return o.t === 'ledge' && o.T !== undefined; }); if (lT.length) { var keepSB = SB; SB = B; try { lT.forEach(function (o) { var q0 = shapeAt(o, 0); if (o.dk) { sbNoBump = true; try { o.hgt = o.T - sbDeckTop(L3_X + q0.x, q0.z, null); } finally { sbNoBump = false; } } else o.hgt = o.T - level3H(L3_X + q0.x, q0.z); }); } finally { SB = keepSB; } } // a ledge with a true Y: its top is at it where the ledge starts (and keeps the ground's shape along it) return B; } function sbDispose() { if (SB) { SB.meshes.forEach(function (m) { scene.remove(m); }); } SB = null; } // ---- Caves: a tunnel (or, from 80 degrees up, a shaft) bored into the ground. The ground is a height map and cannot hold a ceiling over a floor, so where the tunnel is under the surface the surface stays as its roof, the tunnel is a separate swept mesh, and Molly is moved onto the tunnel's own floor while she is inside it. Where the tunnel would break the surface the ground is cut down to its floor. ---- function cavePoly(shape, w, h, roll) { var u = [], k, a; if (shape === 'tri') u = [[-1, -1], [1, -1], [0, 1]]; else if (shape === 'hex') for (k = 0; k < 6; k++) u.push([Math.cos(k * Math.PI / 3), Math.sin(k * Math.PI / 3)]); else if (shape === 'oct') for (k = 0; k < 8; k++) u.push([Math.cos(Math.PI / 8 + k * Math.PI / 4), Math.sin(Math.PI / 8 + k * Math.PI / 4)]); else if (shape === 'circle') for (k = 0; k < 20; k++) u.push([Math.cos(k * Math.PI / 10), Math.sin(k * Math.PI / 10)]); else u = [[-1, -1], [1, -1], [1, 1], [-1, 1]]; a = (roll || 0) * Math.PI / 180; var ca = Math.cos(a), sa = Math.sin(a); return u.map(function (q) { var l = q[0] * w / 2, v = q[1] * h / 2; return [l * ca - v * sa, l * sa + v * ca]; }); } function polyRange(pts, l) { // the lowest and highest points of the cross-section at sideways offset l, or null outside it var lo = 1e9, hi = -1e9, n = pts.length, i, a, b, t, v; for (i = 0; i < n; i++) { a = pts[i]; b = pts[(i + 1) % n]; if (Math.abs(a[0] - b[0]) < 1e-9) { if (Math.abs(a[0] - l) < 1e-6) { lo = Math.min(lo, a[1], b[1]); hi = Math.max(hi, a[1], b[1]); } } else if ((a[0] - l) * (b[0] - l) <= 0) { t = (l - a[0]) / (b[0] - a[0]); v = a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * t; if (v < lo) lo = v; if (v > hi) hi = v; } } return hi < lo ? null : [lo, hi]; } function polySd(pts, x, y) { // signed distance to a convex polygon (negative inside) var n = pts.length, m = -1e9, i, a, b, ex, ey, el, d, sg = 0; for (i = 0; i < n; i++) { a = pts[i]; b = pts[(i + 1) % n]; sg += (b[0] - a[0]) * (b[1] + a[1]); } sg = sg > 0 ? -1 : 1; for (i = 0; i < n; i++) { a = pts[i]; b = pts[(i + 1) % n]; ex = b[0] - a[0]; ey = b[1] - a[1]; el = Math.hypot(ex, ey) || 1; d = sg * ((x - a[0]) * ey - (y - a[1]) * ex) / el; if (d > m) m = d; } return m; } function sbCaveInit() { var B = SB; B.caveOpen = sbHash(8); B.caveCov = sbHash(8); B.caveZone = B.caves.length ? sbHash(8) : null; B.noCave = true; B.caveRuns = []; var byIdC = {}; B.caves.forEach(function (c) { if (c.o.id !== undefined) byIdC[c.o.id] = c; }); function doCave(c) { if (c.done) return; c.done = true; var pv0 = c.o.after !== undefined && byIdC[c.o.after] !== c ? byIdC[c.o.after] : null; if (pv0) doCave(pv0); var joined = pv0 && pv0.end ? pv0.end : null, fromShaft = null; // joined to the end of another tunnel: it carries on from there, the way that one was heading var o = c.o, p = c.p || { x: 0, z: 0, th: 0 }, wx0 = L3_X + p.x, E = joined ? joined.y : o.T !== undefined ? o.T : level3H(wx0, p.z) + (o.y || 0), th = joined ? joined.hd + (o.turn || 0) * Math.PI / 180 : pv0 && pv0.shaft ? pv0.shaft.th + (o.turn || 0) * Math.PI / 180 : p.th, L = Math.max(4, o.len), pts, i, k; // joined: it heads on the way the tunnel ended or the way its shaft faces (plus any turn at the joint), as the editor draws it; its own rotation is not used if (!joined && pv0 && pv0.shaft) { // joined to a shaft: the tunnel starts where its centre line meets the shaft's wall, with its floor level with the shaft's floor var psh = pv0.shaft, sDx = Math.sin(th), sDz = -Math.cos(th), t0 = 0, t1 = psh.rad * 3, tm, qi; for (qi = 0; qi < 30; qi++) { tm = (t0 + t1) / 2; if (polySd(psh.pts, sDx * tm * psh.Lx + sDz * tm * psh.Lz, sDx * tm * psh.Fx + sDz * tm * psh.Fz) <= 0) t0 = tm; else t1 = tm; } p = { x: psh.x + sDx * t0, z: psh.z + sDz * t0, th: th }; E = psh.floor + (o.y || 0); fromShaft = { sh: psh, dx: sDx, dz: sDz, th: th }; } if (joined) { o = {}; for (var ok2 in c.o) o[ok2] = c.o[ok2]; if (!o.angSet) o.ang = Math.min(79, Math.max(-85, joined.pr * 180 / Math.PI)); } // a linked cave carries on at the angle the last one ended on, unless it has its own if (o.ang >= 80) { // a shaft: a pit with the cross-section's outline, straight down pts = cavePoly(o.shape, o.w, o.h, o.roll); var Lx = Math.cos(th), Lz = Math.sin(th), Fx = Math.sin(th), Fz = -Math.cos(th); var pl = pts.map(function (q) { return [q[0], q[1]]; }), rad = 0; pl.forEach(function (q) { rad = Math.max(rad, Math.hypot(q[0], q[1])); }); var cvz = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (+o.carve || 0) / 100)), fTop = E - cvz * L; // carve: how much of the depth the surface slopes in (45 degrees) before the vertical wall begins var sh = { holes: [], x: p.x, z: p.z, th: th, pts: pl, floor: E - L, rad: rad + 4, ext: rad + 4 + (cvz > 0 ? cvz * L + 40 : 0), carve: cvz, fTop: fTop, gr: 1, Lx: Lx, Lz: Lz, Fx: Fx, Fz: Fz, top: E }; B.shafts.push(sh); c.shaft = sh; sh.wall = pl.map(function (q) { var wxp = p.x + Lx * q[0] + Fx * q[1], wzp = p.z + Lz * q[0] + Fz * q[1]; return [wxp, wzp, Math.min(level3H(L3_X + wxp, wzp), cvz > 0 ? fTop : 1e9)]; }); return; } pts = cavePoly(o.shape, o.w, o.h, o.roll); var hwMax = 0, vhiMax = -1e9; pts.forEach(function (q) { hwMax = Math.max(hwMax, Math.abs(q[0])); vhiMax = Math.max(vhiMax, q[1]); }); var r0 = polyRange(pts, 0) || [-o.h / 2, o.h / 2]; var N = Math.max(2, Math.ceil(L / 1.5)), ds = L / N, bend = o.bend * Math.PI / 180, S = [], cur = null; function frame(sv) { var hd = th + bend * sv / L, pr = Math.max(-85, Math.min(85, o.ang + o.slope * sv / L)) * Math.PI / 180, cp = Math.cos(pr), sp = Math.sin(pr); return { hd: hd, pr: pr, cp: cp, sp: sp, tn: Math.tan(pr), Tx: Math.sin(hd) * cp, Ty: -sp, Tz: -Math.cos(hd) * cp, Lx: Math.cos(hd), Lz: Math.sin(hd), Nx: Math.sin(hd) * sp, Ny: cp, Nz: -Math.cos(hd) * sp }; } var f0 = frame(0), A = joined ? { x: joined.x, y: joined.y, z: joined.z } : { x: p.x - f0.Nx * r0[0], y: E - f0.Ny * r0[0], z: p.z - f0.Nz * r0[0] }; for (k = 0; k <= N; k++) { var f = frame(k * ds); f.x = A.x; f.y = A.y; f.z = A.z; var tx = A.x + f.Nx * vhiMax, tz = A.z + f.Nz * vhiMax, top = A.y + f.Ny * vhiMax, surf = level3H(L3_X + tx, tz) + sbLedge(L3_X + tx, tz); f.cov = (k > 0 || !!joined || !!fromShaft) && top < surf - 0.5; f.surf = surf; f.fy = A.y + f.Ny * r0[0]; f.k = k; var hl = Math.hypot(f.Tx, f.Tz) || 1e-6; f.Thx = f.Tx / hl; f.Thz = f.Tz / hl; f.dh = ds * f.cp; f.hw = hwMax; f.pts = pts; f.cp0 = f.cp; S.push(f); if (k < N) A = { x: A.x + f.Tx * ds, y: A.y + f.Ty * ds, z: A.z + f.Tz * ds }; } if (fromShaft) fromShaft.sh.holes.push({ S0: S[0], pts: pts, hw: hwMax, dx: fromShaft.dx, dz: fromShaft.dz, th: fromShaft.th }); c.end = { x: A.x, y: A.y, z: A.z, hd: th + bend, pr: frame(L).pr }; c.S = S; if (joined && pv0.S) { // the joint between two tunnels: sbCaveQ eases the floor and roof from one to the other across it, over the samples within CAVE_JW of it on either side var pS = pv0.S, jn = { P: pS[pS.length - 1], Q: S[0] }, jk; for (jk = pS.length - 1; jk >= 0 && Math.hypot(pS[jk].x - jn.P.x, pS[jk].z - jn.P.z) < CAVE_JW + pS[jk].dh; jk--) pS[jk].jn = jn; for (jk = 0; jk < S.length && Math.hypot(S[jk].x - jn.Q.x, S[jk].z - jn.Q.z) < CAVE_JW + S[jk].dh; jk++) S[jk].jn = jn; } S.forEach(function (f) { if (f.cov) sbAdd(B.caveCov, f.x, f.z, f); else if (f.fy < f.surf - 0.2) sbAdd(B.caveOpen, f.x, f.z, f); }); var zin = {}; for (k = 0; k < S.length; k++) if (S[k].cov) { for (var zk = Math.max(0, k - 3); zk <= k; zk++) if (!S[zk].cov || zk === k) zin[zk] = 1; zin[k] = 1; if (k + 1 < S.length && !S[k + 1].cov) for (var zk2 = k + 1; zk2 <= Math.min(S.length - 1, k + 3); zk2++) if (!S[zk2].cov) zin[zk2] = 1; } for (var zz in zin) sbAdd(B.caveZone, S[zz].x, S[zz].z, S[zz]); // the mouth: where the tunnel goes under the surface B.caveR = Math.max(B.caveR, hwMax + 6); for (k = 0; k < S.length; k++) if (S[k].cov && (k === 0 || !S[k - 1].cov)) { var e1 = k; while (e1 + 1 < S.length && S[e1 + 1].cov) e1++; B.caveRuns.push(c.lastRun = { S: S, a: Math.max(0, k - 1), b: Math.min(S.length - 1, e1 + 1), pts: pts, hw: hwMax, endsCov: e1 === S.length - 1 }); k = e1; } if (joined && pv0.lastRun) pv0.lastRun.endsCov = false; // no end wall where another tunnel carries on } B.caves.forEach(doCave); B.noCave = false; } function sbCaveQuery(x, z) { return sbCaveQ(SB && SB.caveCov, x, z); } var CAVE_JW = 3; // how far either side of the joint between two tunnels the floor and roof ease from one to the other function sbCaveFR(f, ex, z) { // the floor and roof of a tunnel sample's own stretch at (ex, z), carried on straight past it var dx = ex - f.x, dz = z - f.z, al = dx * f.Thx + dz * f.Thz, rg = polyRange(f.pts, Math.max(-f.hw, Math.min(f.hw, dx * f.Lx + dz * f.Lz))); if (!rg) return null; return [f.y + rg[0] / f.cp - f.tn * al, f.y + rg[1] / f.cp - f.tn * al]; } function sbCaveQ(hash, x, z, slack) { // the tunnel floor and roof under (x, z), where a stretch of tunnel is (hash: the covered samples, or the mouth zone) var B = SB; if (!B || !hash) return null; var ex = x - L3_X, best = null, bd = 1e9; slack = slack || 0; sbNear(hash, ex, z, B.caveR, function (e) { var dx = ex - e.x, dz = z - e.z, al = dx * e.Thx + dz * e.Thz; if (Math.abs(al) > e.dh * 0.6 + 0.01) return; var lat = dx * e.Lx + dz * e.Lz; if (Math.abs(lat) > e.hw + slack) return; var ab = Math.abs(al); if (ab < bd) { bd = ab; best = { e: e, lat: lat, al: al }; } }); if (!best) return null; var e2 = best.e, rg = polyRange(e2.pts, Math.max(-e2.hw, Math.min(e2.hw, best.lat))); if (!rg) return null; best.fl = e2.y + rg[0] / e2.cp - e2.tn * best.al; best.rf = e2.y + rg[1] / e2.cp - e2.tn * best.al; best.rg = rg; // straight down through the tilted cross-section: a section height of rg is rg / cos(angle) tall upright, as the tunnel is drawn if (e2.jn) { // near the joint between two tunnels of different angles (or shapes) each one's own floor and roof would stop at a step: ease from one to the other over CAVE_JW either side, so she can run across it var eP = e2.jn.P, eQ = e2.jn.Q, jx = eP.Thx + eQ.Thx, jz = eP.Thz + eQ.Thz, jl = Math.hypot(jx, jz) || 1, ja = ((ex - eP.x) * jx + (z - eP.z) * jz) / jl; if (ja > -CAVE_JW && ja < CAVE_JW) { var jt = smooth01((ja + CAVE_JW) / (2 * CAVE_JW)), fP = sbCaveFR(eP, ex, z), fQ = sbCaveFR(eQ, ex, z); if (fP && fQ) { best.fl = fP[0] + (fQ[0] - fP[0]) * jt; best.rf = fP[1] + (fQ[1] - fP[1]) * jt; } } } return best; } function sbShaftIn(x, z, slack) { // the shaft whose outline (grown by slack) holds the world point (x, z), or null var B = SB; if (!B || !B.shafts.length) return null; var ex = x - L3_X; for (var i = 0; i < B.shafts.length; i++) { var sh = B.shafts[i], dx = ex - sh.x, dz = z - sh.z, r = sh.rad + (slack > 0 ? slack : 0); if (dx > r || dx < -r || dz > r || dz < -r) continue; if (polySd(sh.pts, dx * sh.Lx + dz * sh.Lz, dx * sh.Fx + dz * sh.Fz) <= slack) return sh; } return null; } function sbCaveCarve(ex, z, y) { // ground cut down to the tunnel's floor where the tunnel breaks the surface var B = SB, out = y; if (B.caveOpen) sbNear(B.caveOpen, ex, z, B.caveR, function (e) { var dx = ex - e.x, dz = z - e.z, al = dx * e.Thx + dz * e.Thz; if (Math.abs(al) > e.dh * 0.5 + 0.02) return; var lat = Math.abs(dx * e.Lx + dz * e.Lz), k = lat <= e.hw ? 1 : 1 - smooth01((lat - e.hw) / 2.5); if (k <= 0) return; var v = y + (e.fy - y) * k; if (v < out) out = v; }); for (var i = 0; i < B.shafts.length; i++) { // a shaft's wall is exact (see groundH). With carve, the surface slopes in toward the outline at 45 degrees and meets the natural ground wherever that is: no overhang var sh = B.shafts[i]; if (!sh.carve) continue; var dx2 = ex - sh.x, dz2 = z - sh.z; if (dx2 > sh.ext || dx2 < -sh.ext || dz2 > sh.ext || dz2 < -sh.ext) continue; var v2 = sh.fTop + sh.gr * polySd(sh.pts, dx2 * sh.Lx + dz2 * sh.Lz, dx2 * sh.Fx + dz2 * sh.Fz); if (v2 < out) out = v2; } return out; } function sbCaveMeshes() { var B = SB, mat = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ vertexColors: true, flatShading: true, side: THREE.DoubleSide }), col = new THREE.Color(), tc = 0; function rock(P, C, a, b, c, k) { var j = rand01(tc, 3, 61), j2 = rand01(tc, 9, 62); tc++; col.setHSL(0.08 + (j - 0.5) * 0.015, 0.14 + (j - 0.5) * 0.05, (0.44 + (j - 0.5) * 0.08 + (j2 - 0.5) * 0.03) * k); [a, b, c].forEach(function (v) { P.push(v[0], v[1], v[2]); C.push(col.r, col.g, col.b); }); } function make(P, C) { if (!P.length) return; var g = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(P), 3)); g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(C), 3)); g.computeVertexNormals(); var m = new THREE.Mesh(g, mat); m.castShadow = true; m.receiveShadow = true; scene.add(m); B.meshes.push(m); } var P = [], C = []; B.caveRuns.forEach(function (run) { var S = run.S, pts = run.pts, n = pts.length, k, j; function V(f, q) { return [L3_X + f.x + f.Lx * q[0] + f.Nx * q[1], f.y + f.Ny * q[1], f.z + f.Lz * q[0] + f.Nz * q[1]]; } for (k = run.a; k < run.b; k++) for (j = 0; j < n; j++) { var a0 = V(S[k], pts[j]), a1 = V(S[k], pts[(j + 1) % n]), b0 = V(S[k + 1], pts[j]), b1 = V(S[k + 1], pts[(j + 1) % n]), sh = pts[j][1] < -0.2 * 1 ? 0.9 : 1; rock(P, C, a0, a1, b1, sh); rock(P, C, a0, b1, b0, sh); } var y0 = 1e9, y1 = -1e9; pts.forEach(function (q) { y0 = Math.min(y0, q[1]); y1 = Math.max(y1, q[1]); }); if (run.a === 0 || !S[run.a - 1] || true) for (k = Math.max(0, run.a - 3); k < run.a; k++) { // the lip: the tunnel's floor carries on out of the mouth, a hair under the carved ground, so no gap shows where the cut edge meets the floor if (S[k].cov) continue; for (j = 0; j < n; j++) { if ((pts[j][1] + pts[(j + 1) % n][1]) / 2 > y0 + (y1 - y0) * 0.15) continue; var g0 = V(S[k], pts[j]), g1 = V(S[k], pts[(j + 1) % n]), h0 = V(S[k + 1], pts[j]), h1 = V(S[k + 1], pts[(j + 1) % n]); [g0, g1, h0, h1].forEach(function (v) { v[1] -= 0.04; }); rock(P, C, g0, g1, h1, 0.9); rock(P, C, g0, h1, h0, 0.9); } } if (run.endsCov) { var fe = S[run.b], cx = 0, cy = 0; pts.forEach(function (q) { cx += q[0] / n; cy += q[1] / n; }); var ctr = V(fe, [cx, cy]); for (j = 0; j < n; j++) rock(P, C, V(fe, pts[j]), V(fe, pts[(j + 1) % n]), ctr, 0.8); } }); make(P, C); P = []; C = []; B.shafts.forEach(function (sh) { // a shaft's walls, from the floor up to the ground at each corner, and its floor var n = sh.pts.length, j, fcx = 0, fcz = 0; sh.wall.forEach(function (w) { fcx += w[0] / n; fcz += w[1] / n; }); for (j = 0; j < n; j++) { var fa = sh.wall[j], fb = sh.wall[(j + 1) % n]; rock(P, C, [L3_X + fa[0], sh.floor, fa[1]], [L3_X + fb[0], sh.floor, fb[1]], [L3_X + fcx, sh.floor, fcz], 0.7); } for (j = 0; j < n; j++) { var a = sh.wall[j], b = sh.wall[(j + 1) % n], el = Math.hypot(b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]), nc = sh.holes.length ? Math.max(1, Math.ceil(el)) : 1, ci; for (ci = 0; ci < nc; ci++) { // the wall in strips, so a tunnel joined to the shaft can have its opening cut out of it var u0 = ci / nc, u1 = (ci + 1) / nc, x0 = a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * u0, z0 = a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * u0, x1 = a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * u1, z1 = a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * u1; var t0 = a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * u0 + 0.3, t1 = a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * u1 + 0.3, mxp = (x0 + x1) / 2 - sh.x, mzp = (z0 + z1) / 2 - sh.z, hy0 = null, hy1 = null, hi; for (hi = 0; hi < sh.holes.length && hy0 === null; hi++) { var ho = sh.holes[hi], hl = mxp * Math.cos(ho.th) + mzp * Math.sin(ho.th), hf = mxp * ho.dx + mzp * ho.dz; if (hf > 0 && Math.abs(hl) < ho.hw - 0.05) { var hr = polyRange(ho.pts, hl); if (hr) { hy0 = ho.S0.y + hr[0] / ho.S0.cp; hy1 = ho.S0.y + hr[1] / ho.S0.cp; } } } function strip(ylo0, ylo1, yhi0, yhi1) { rock(P, C, [L3_X + x0, ylo0, z0], [L3_X + x1, ylo1, z1], [L3_X + x1, yhi1, z1], 0.85); rock(P, C, [L3_X + x0, ylo0, z0], [L3_X + x1, yhi1, z1], [L3_X + x0, yhi0, z0], 0.85); } if (hy0 === null) strip(sh.floor, sh.floor, t0, t1); else { if (hy0 > sh.floor + 0.01) strip(sh.floor, sh.floor, hy0, hy0); if (hy1 < Math.min(t0, t1) - 0.01) strip(hy1, hy1, t0, t1); } } } }); make(P, C); } // Oceana Crags (Sandbox ground 'level2'): the path is not part of the ground at all. It is a deck of its own: an exact strip, drawn by sbMakeMeshes and stood on through sbDeckTop, with the ground under it left as it is. Her floor is whichever is higher, the deck or the ground, so what she stands on and what is drawn are the same surface by construction var DECK_TH = 2, DECK_CUT = 0.3, DECK_EDGE = 4.5; // a deck's thickness where the segment gives no slab thickness; how far under a superseding deck taller rock is cut down; and how far past the deck's edges that cut stays full before it eases back up (a grid cell and its jitter, so no rock pokes through the deck's edge) var l3Tx = 0, l3Ps = 0, l3Pu = 0; // the path surface nearest the last ground point read: its texture (0 stone, 1 wood planks, 2 metal plates, 3 grass), how far along the path and how far across it var l3Bk = 0, l3A = 0, l3P = -Infinity, l3N = Infinity, l3S0 = 0, ledI = NaN, ledA = 0, ledCA = 0, ledCI = NaN, ledTox = false; // what the last ground height was built from: stacked bumps, the highest and lowest independent ones, and what was added in all function sbComb(hA, P, N, La, Li) { // ground rise at a corner: stacked bumps plus stacked ledges, but an independent bump or ledge only reaches its own height and merges into whatever is higher (or lower, for a trench) var S = hA + La; if (P > S) S = P; if (Li > 0 && Li > S) S = Li; if (N < S) S = N; if (Li < 0 && Li < S) S = Li; return S; } function cornerY(x, z, L, La, LI) { // ground height at a mesh corner for a cell whose ledge is L (La and LI: its stacked and independent parts) var y = worldH0(x, z); if (SB && SB.indep && level === 3 && x > L3_MIN) return y + sbComb(l3A, l3P, l3N, La, LI) - l3S0; return y + L; } var GH_X = 0, GH_Z = -9000, GH_R = 9000, GH_H = 3000; // the Giant Hill: centred 9,000 units ahead of the start, 9,000 in radius and 3,000 high, so its foot is where the path begins and its flanks climb to about 27 degrees function level3H(x, z) { wSt = 0; wPl = 0; wMud = 0; wF = 0; wL2 = 0; l3A = 0; l3P = -Infinity; l3N = Infinity; l3S0 = 0; l3Bk = 0; l3Tx = 0; if (!SB) return 0; var ex = x - L3_X, y = 0, B = SB, HW = SB.hw, hwq = SB.hw, best = 1e9, py = 0, ing = false, tox = false, pov = false, i, d; sbNear(B.gapH, ex, z, B.maxG, function (e) { if (!ing && sbIn(e, ex, z, 0)) ing = true; }); var bp = null; sbNear(B.pathH, ex, z, B.hwMax + 18, function (p) { if (p.fl || p.wl || p.dsk) return; var dd = Math.hypot(p.x - ex, p.z - z); if (dd < best) { best = dd; py = p.y; bp = p; } }); if (bp) { // the height is read along the path between its samples, not off the nearest one, so a steep rise is a smooth slope and not steps var sm0 = B.samples, nb, ta, bd2 = 1e9, tt, qx, qz, pa, pb2, vx, vz, vl, bkq = 0; for (var di = -1; di <= 0; di++) { pa = sm0[bp.i + di]; pb2 = sm0[bp.i + di + 1]; if (!pa || !pb2 || pb2.brk || pb2.fl || (pb2.wl && pa.wl) || (pa.dsk && pb2.dsk)) continue; // a floating piece does not shape the ground under it vx = pb2.x - pa.x; vz = pb2.z - pa.z; vl = vx * vx + vz * vz; if (vl < 1e-9) continue; var tr = ((ex - pa.x) * vx + (z - pa.z) * vz) / vl; tt = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, tr)); qx = pa.x + vx * tt; qz = pa.z + vz * tt; var dq = Math.hypot(ex - qx, z - qz); if (dq < bd2) { hwq = ((pa.w || B.width) + ((pb2.w || B.width) - (pa.w || B.width)) * tt) / 2; bkq = 0; bd2 = dq; best = dq; pov = !!pb2.pv; py = pa.y + (pb2.y - pa.y) * tt; l3Tx = pb2.tx || 0; l3Ps = pa.s + (pb2.s - pa.s) * tt; l3Pu = ((ex - pa.x) * -vz + (z - pa.z) * vx) / Math.sqrt(vl); var rlq = (pa.roll || 0) + ((pb2.roll || 0) - (pa.roll || 0)) * tt; if (rlq > 0.05 || rlq < -0.05) { var latq = ((ex - pa.x) * -vz + (z - pa.z) * vx) / Math.sqrt(vl) + (pb2.th - pa.th) * Math.sqrt(vl) * tt * (1 - tt) / 2; /* the samples sit on the true curve but the chord between them cuts inside it (by up to length squared over eight radii), and a steep bank turns that into a bump at every sample: the arc's own bulge is added back. Also how far across the chord she is, measured square to the chord itself: reading it off the blended heading was a little out on a curve, and a steep bank multiplies that error */ var cq0 = Math.cos(rlq * Math.PI / 180); var hw0q = hwq; hwq *= cq0; py -= Math.max(-hwq, Math.min(hwq, latq)) * Math.tan(rlq * Math.PI / 180); if ((rlq > 20 || rlq < -20) && dq <= hw0q + 3) bkq = 1; } // a banked path keeps its width measured along the slope: across the ground it is only cos(bank) as wide, and rises width x sin(bank) // a banked stretch: the right side lower for a positive bank } } } l3Bk = B.terrain === 'level2' ? 0 : bkq || 0; // a steeply banked stretch is within reach of this point: its ground needs a finer grid than the usual cell (not on Oceana, where a banked path is an exact deck and the ground is left alone) HW = hwq; for (i = 0; i < B.discs.length; i++) { // a circular deck: where its rim is nearer than the path's edge, it is the path here (its centre and radius stand in for the nearest path point and half width, so the ground eases to it the same way) var Dk = B.discs[i], ddx = ex - Dk.cx, ddz = z - Dk.cz, ddd = Math.hypot(ddx, ddz); if (ddd > Dk.R + 30 || ddd - Dk.R >= best - HW) continue; var dcl = ddd > Dk.R ? Dk.R / ddd : 1; best = ddd; HW = Dk.R; py = sbDiscY(Dk, Dk.cx + ddx * dcl, Dk.cz + ddz * dcl); pov = Dk.ov; l3Tx = Dk.tx; bp = bp || Dk; l3Ps = ddx * Math.sin(Dk.hh) - ddz * Math.cos(Dk.hh); l3Pu = ddx * Math.cos(Dk.hh) + ddz * Math.sin(Dk.hh); } if (ing) { best = 1e9; py = 0; } // a gap is a break in the path: the ground is whatever is under it, with no path across if (B.terrain === 'grass') { // Grass Canyon's rolling ground and nothing else: no walls, ledges or plateau, going on for ever. The path is laid over it and the ground eases into it var gb = terrainFlat(ex, z); y = gb; if (best < HW + 14) { var gk = 1 - smooth01((best - HW) / 14); y = gb + (py - gb) * gk; if (best <= HW) wL2 = 1; } } else if (B.terrain === 'crags') { // the jagged rock of Oceana Crags and nothing else: spikes and crags rising from a flat floor, with a clear lane along the path that the rock eases down to var cenv = smooth01((best - HW - 10) / 50), csp = mtSpikes(ex, z) * cenv; y = Math.min(300, csp + (rand01(Math.round(ex / GRID), Math.round(z / GRID), 36) - 0.5) * 6 * Math.min(1, csp / 40)); if (csp > 4) wSt = 1; if (best < HW + 14) { var ck = 1 - smooth01((best - HW) / 14); y += (py - y) * ck; if (best <= HW) { wL2 = 1; wSt = 0; } } } else if (B.terrain === 'giant') { // one enormous hill, smooth and wide. The path rides on it: its height (the Rise) is measured from the hill's own surface var gdd = Math.hypot(ex - GH_X, z - GH_Z), ghv = GH_H * smooth01(1 - gdd / GH_R) + terrainFlat(ex, z); y = ghv; if (best < HW + 14) { var gk2 = 1 - smooth01((best - HW) / 14); y = ghv + py * gk2; if (best <= HW) wL2 = 1; } } else if (B.terrain === 'ocean') { // Oceana Crags' open sea and nothing else: no ground at all, the path is a causeway over it. She falls through the water to the bottom of the world y = L2_VOID; if (best < HW + 2) { y = L2_VOID + (py - L2_VOID) * (1 - smooth01((best - HW) / 2)); if (best <= HW) wL2 = 1; } } else if (B.terrain === 'level2') { // Oceana Crags' own generated ground (it sets the colour flags too). The path here is a deck of its own, not part of the ground y = worldH0(L2_X + ex, z + L2_SBZ); if (bp && best < 1e8) { if (pov && y > py - DECK_CUT) { var pkc = 1 - smooth01((best - HW - DECK_EDGE) / 14); if (pkc > 0) { y += (py - DECK_CUT - y) * pkc; wL2 = 0; wSt = 1; wPl = 0; wMud = 0; } } // this piece supersedes the terrain: rock standing higher than the deck is cut down to just under it, a little wider than the deck, easing back up to the terrain over 14 units. Lower ground is never touched } } else if (B.terrain) { // Grass Canyon's own generated ground (it sets the colour flags too) y = worldH0(ex, z); if (pov) { var pk2 = 1 - smooth01((best - HW) / 14); if (pk2 > 0) { y += (py - y) * pk2; if (best <= HW) { wL2 = 1; wSt = 0; wPl = 0; wMud = 0; } } } // this piece is set to win over the terrain: the ground is cut down or built up to it, easing back to the terrain over 14 units else if (best <= HW && y < py - 0.3) { y = py; wL2 = 1; wSt = 0; wPl = 0; wMud = 0; } // the path over low ground is a slab, as in the real level } else if (best < HW + 14) { y = py * (1 - smooth01((best - HW) / 14)); if (best <= HW) wL2 = 1; } for (i = 0; i < B.patches.length; i++) { // terrain patches: their own ground over the level's, with a hard or a soft edge var pa = B.patches[i]; if (Math.abs(pa.x - ex) > pa.rr || Math.abs(pa.z - z) > pa.rr) continue; var pc = Math.cos(-pa.th), ps = Math.sin(-pa.th), pdx = ex - pa.x, pdz = z - pa.z, plx = pdx * pc - pdz * ps, plz = pdx * ps + pdz * pc, pdist; if (pa.shape === 'rect') pdist = Math.min(pa.hw - Math.abs(plx), pa.hd - Math.abs(plz)); else pdist = (1 - Math.hypot(plx / pa.hw, plz / pa.hd)) * Math.min(pa.hw, pa.hd); var pk = pa.hard ? (pdist >= 0 ? 1 : 0) : smooth01((pdist + pa.soft / 2) / pa.soft); if (pk <= 0) continue; var pyy, dkG = B.terrain === 'level2', onP = best <= HW && !dkG, pyP = dkG ? py - DECK_CUT : py; // (on Oceana the path is a deck of its own: a patch's ground eases to just under it, and is not painted as path) if (pa.ground === 'ocean') { pyy = L2_VOID; if (best < HW + 2 && !dkG) pyy = L2_VOID + (py - L2_VOID) * (1 - smooth01((best - HW) / 2)); } else if (pa.ground === 'flat') pyy = best < HW + 14 ? pyP * (1 - smooth01((best - HW) / 14)) : 0; else { var pgb = terrainFlat(ex, z); pyy = best < HW + 14 ? pgb + (pyP - pgb) * (1 - smooth01((best - HW) / 14)) : pgb; } if (pa.T !== undefined && pa.ground !== 'ocean') pyy = pa.T; // a true Y: the patch's ground is at it y += (pyy - y) * pk; if (pk >= 0.5) { wSt = 0; wPl = 0; wMud = 0; wF = 0; wL2 = onP ? 1 : pa.ground === 'toxic' ? 3 : 0; } } var cone = 0, coneI = 0, hA = 0, hv; sbNear(B.coneH, ex, z, 16, function (c) { if (c.T !== undefined) return; var dd = Math.hypot(c.x - ex, c.z - z); if (dd < c.rad) { if (c.ind) coneI = Math.max(coneI, c.h * (1 - dd / c.rad)); else cone = Math.max(cone, c.h * (1 - dd / c.rad)); } }); if (cone > 0 || coneI > 0) { hA = cone; if (coneI > l3P) l3P = coneI; wL2 = 0; if (cone > 0.5 || coneI > 0.5) wSt = 1; } for (i = 0; i < B.hills.length; i++) { var h = B.hills[i]; if (h.T !== undefined) continue; d = Math.hypot(ex - h.x, z - h.z); if (d < h.r) { hv = h.h * (1 - smooth01(d / h.r)); if (h.ind) { if (hv >= 0) { if (hv > l3P) l3P = hv; } else if (hv < l3N) l3N = hv; } else hA += hv; wL2 = 0; if (h.rock === 'rock' && d < h.r * 0.75) wSt = 1; } } l3A = hA; l3S0 = hA; if (l3P > l3S0) l3S0 = l3P; if (l3N < l3S0) l3S0 = l3N; y += l3S0; for (i = 0; i < B.hillsT.length; i++) { var hT = B.hillsT[i]; d = Math.hypot(ex - hT.x, z - hT.z); if (d < hT.r) { var hkT = 1 - smooth01((d / hT.r - 0.85) / 0.15); y += (hT.T + hT.h * (1 - smooth01(d / hT.r)) - y) * hkT; wL2 = 0; if (hT.rock === 'rock' && d < hT.r * 0.75) wSt = 1; } } // a hill with a true Y: built up from that height, not from the ground sbNear(B.coneH, ex, z, 16, function (c) { if (c.T === undefined) return; var dd = Math.hypot(c.x - ex, c.z - z); if (dd < c.rad) { var cs = c.T + c.h * (1 - dd / c.rad); if (cs > y) { y = cs; wL2 = 0; if (c.h * (1 - dd / c.rad) > 0.5) wSt = 1; } } }); // a crag with a true Y: its rocks rise from that height for (i = 0; i < B.lakes.length; i++) { var lk = B.lakes[i]; d = Math.hypot(ex - lk.x, z - lk.z); if (d < lk.r) { y -= lk.depth * (1 - smooth01((d - lk.r * 0.7) / (lk.r * 0.3))); if (d < lk.r * 0.9) { wSt = 1; wL2 = 0; } } } if (B.slabH) { var scut = sbSlabCut(ex, z, y); if (scut < y) { y = scut; wSt = 1; wL2 = 0; wPl = 0; wMud = 0; } } // a floating slab spans a drop: any higher ground in its way is cut down to just under it if (!B.noCave && B.caves.length) { var Lp = sbLedge(x, z), cy0 = sbCaveCarve(ex, z, y + Lp) - Lp; if (cy0 < y) { y = cy0; wSt = 1; wL2 = 0; wPl = 0; wMud = 0; } } for (i = 0; i < (sbNoRamps || B.terrain === 'level2' ? 0 : B.ramps.length); i++) { // (on Oceana a ramp is a wedge of its own on the path's deck: see sbDeckTop) var rp = B.ramps[i]; if (Math.abs(rp.x - ex) > rp.rr || Math.abs(rp.z - z) > rp.rr) continue; var dx = ex - rp.x, dz = z - rp.z, lf = dx * Math.sin(rp.th) - dz * Math.cos(rp.th), lx = dx * Math.cos(rp.th) + dz * Math.sin(rp.th); if (Math.abs(lx) <= rp.hw && lf >= -rp.hl && lf <= rp.hl) { y = (rp.base === undefined ? y : rp.base) + rp.tn * (lf + rp.hl) + rp.y; wL2 = 1; } // a flat plane rising from the height she stands on at its foot (the path, a slab of path, or the ground there), the same for the ground mesh and for her feet } var tca = sbToxAt(B, ex, z); if (tca) for (i = 0; i < tca.length; i++) { var t = tca[i]; if (Math.abs(t.x - ex) > t.rr || Math.abs(t.z - z) > t.rr) continue; var tdx = ex - t.x, tdz = z - t.z, lx2 = tdx * t.cn - tdz * t.sn, lz2 = tdx * t.sn + tdz * t.cn; if (Math.abs(lx2) <= t.hw && Math.abs(lz2) <= t.hd) { tox = true; break; } } { var mud = false, mudT; sbNear(B.mudH, ex, z, B.maxM, function (e) { if (!mud && sbIn(e, ex, z, 0)) { mud = true; mudT = e.o.T; } }); if (mud) { if (mudT !== undefined) y = mudT; wMud = 1; wL2 = 0; } } // mud with a true Y: the ground under it is at that height return y; } function sbLedge(x, z) { if (!SB) return 0; var best = 0, bi = 0, bt = false, bit = false, ex = x - L3_X, base = 0; ledTox = false; if (SB.terrain === 'level1' || SB.terrain === 'level2') base = SB.terrain === 'level2' ? ledgeH(L2_X + ex, z + L2_SBZ) : ledgeH(ex, z); sbNear(SB.ledgeH, ex, z, SB.maxL, function (e) { if (sbIn(e, ex, z, 0)) { if (e.o.ind) { if (Math.abs(e.o.hgt) > Math.abs(bi)) { bi = e.o.hgt; bit = !!e.o.tox; } } else if (Math.abs(e.o.hgt) > Math.abs(best)) { best = e.o.hgt; bt = !!e.o.tox; } } }); ledA = base + best; ledI = bi === 0 ? NaN : bi; ledTox = ledI === ledI && Math.abs(ledI) > Math.abs(best) ? bit : bt; // ledTox: the ledge whose top this is has a toxic surface return ledI === ledI && Math.abs(ledI) > Math.abs(best) ? ledI + (base ? base : 0) : base + best; // for anything that only wants one number: the taller of the two } function sbDoorSpan(m, ex, z) { // where the vertical line through (ex, z) passes through the door's slab right now: its lowest and highest world height there (null if it misses). The slab is tilted about its base, forward-and-back (tx) and side to side (tz) var th = m.th0, dx = ex - m.cx - m.sx, dz = z - m.cz - m.sz, X = dx * Math.cos(th) + dz * Math.sin(th), Z = dx * Math.sin(th) - dz * Math.cos(th); var ct = Math.cos(m.tz), st = Math.sin(m.tz), cp = Math.cos(m.tx), sp = Math.sin(m.tx), y0 = m.base + m.dy, lo = -1e9, hi = 1e9, i, a, b, l, h, y1, y2, cs = [ [X * ct, -st, -m.half, m.half], [X * st * cp + Z * sp, ct * cp, 0, m.hgt], [-X * st * sp + Z * cp, -ct * sp, -m.depth / 2, m.depth / 2]]; // each local coordinate of the slab, as (a + b * height above its base), with its range for (i = 0; i < 3; i++) { a = cs[i][0]; b = cs[i][1]; l = cs[i][2]; h = cs[i][3]; if (Math.abs(b) < 1e-6) { if (a < l || a > h) return null; continue; } y1 = (l - a) / b; y2 = (h - a) / b; if (y1 > y2) { var sw = y1; y1 = y2; y2 = sw; } if (y1 > lo) lo = y1; if (y2 < hi) hi = y2; } if (lo > hi) return null; return { th: th, la: X, al: Z, lo: y0 + lo, hi: y0 + hi }; } function sbMovInside(m, ex, z) { // is (ex, z) over this platform right now? Returns its sample frame, or null var px = ex - m.off[0], pz = z - m.off[1]; if (px < m.minx || px > m.maxx || pz < m.minz || pz > m.maxz) return null; if (m.door) return sbDoorSpan(m, ex, z); for (var k = 0; k < m.pts.length; k++) { var q = m.pts[k], dx = px - q[0], dz = pz - q[1], th = q[2], al = dx * Math.sin(th) - dz * Math.cos(th), la = dx * Math.cos(th) + dz * Math.sin(th); if (Math.abs(la) <= m.half && Math.abs(al) <= 1.15) return { th: th, la: la }; } return null; } function sbMoverTop(x, z, py) { // the top of whichever platform is under (x, z) right now that she is on or above (py = her feet; null: any), or a very low number var B = SB, ex = x - L3_X, top = -1e9; for (var i = 0; i < B.movers.length; i++) { var m = B.movers[i], t; if (m.door) { var ds = sbMovInside(m, ex, z); if (!ds) continue; t = ds.hi; if (t <= top || (py !== null && py < t - 1.2)) continue; top = t; continue; } t = m.base + m.hgt + m.dy; if (t <= top || (py !== null && py < t - 1.2)) continue; if (sbMovInside(m, ex, z)) top = t; } return top; } function sbDoorPose(m, force) { // put the door's eight corners where its slab is now var th = m.th0, c = Math.cos(th), sn = Math.sin(th), ct = Math.cos(m.tz), st = Math.sin(m.tz), cp = Math.cos(m.tx), sp = Math.sin(m.tx), y0 = m.base + m.dy, V = [], i, P = m.geo.attributes.position.array; for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) { var lx = (i & 1 ? 1 : -1) * m.half, ly = (i & 2 ? 1 : 0) * m.hgt, lz = (i & 4 ? 1 : -1) * m.depth / 2; var y1 = ly * cp - lz * sp, z1 = ly * sp + lz * cp, x2 = lx * ct + y1 * st, y2 = -lx * st + y1 * ct; // pitch, then roll (lat, up, forward) V.push([L3_X + m.cx + m.sx + x2 * c + z1 * sn, y0 + y2, m.cz + m.sz + x2 * sn - z1 * c]); } var F = [[0, 1, 3, 2], [4, 6, 7, 5], [0, 2, 6, 4], [1, 5, 7, 3], [2, 3, 7, 6], [0, 4, 5, 1]], k = 0; F.forEach(function (q) { [q[0], q[1], q[2], q[0], q[2], q[3]].forEach(function (vi) { P[k++] = V[vi][0]; P[k++] = V[vi][1]; P[k++] = V[vi][2]; }); }); m.geo.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true; m.geo.computeVertexNormals(); m.geo.computeBoundingSphere(); } function sbDoorLinked(m) { // has everything this door is linked to been beaten? (a mob that is not on the map at all counts as not beaten) var ids = m.o.links, i, e, found; if (!ids.length) return false; for (i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) { var tr = SB.trig && SB.trig[ids[i]]; if (tr) { if (!tr.on) return false; continue; } // a pressure plate (pressed) or a target (broken) found = false; for (var q = 0; q < MOBS.length; q++) { e = MOBS[q]; if (e.sbp && e.sbId === ids[i]) { found = true; if (!e.dead) return false; } } if (!found) return false; } return true; } var sbNoBump = false; function sbDeckLedge(ex, z) { // Oceana: how far the ledges standing on a path deck raise its top at (ex, z) (the taller of the stacked and the independent ones, as on the ground; a dip goes no deeper than the deck) var B = SB, best = 0, bi = 0; if (!B.dLedgeH) return 0; sbNear(B.dLedgeH, ex, z, B.maxL, function (e) { if (sbIn(e, ex, z, 0)) { if (e.o.ind) { if (Math.abs(e.o.hgt) > Math.abs(bi)) bi = e.o.hgt; } else if (Math.abs(e.o.hgt) > Math.abs(best)) best = e.o.hgt; } }); return Math.max(-(DECK_TH - 0.2), Math.abs(bi) > Math.abs(best) ? bi : best); } function sbDeckHill(ex, z, y) { // Oceana: the deck's top at (ex, z) with the hills standing on it (y: the deck's own top there). A hill with a true Y is built up from that height, as on the ground var B = SB, i, h, d, add = 0; for (i = 0; i < B.dHills.length; i++) { h = B.dHills[i]; if (h.T !== undefined) continue; d = Math.hypot(ex - h.x, z - h.z); if (d < h.r) add += h.h * (1 - smooth01(d / h.r)); } y += Math.max(-(DECK_TH - 0.2), add); for (i = 0; i < B.dHills.length; i++) { h = B.dHills[i]; if (h.T === undefined) continue; d = Math.hypot(ex - h.x, z - h.z); if (d < h.r) y += (h.T + h.h * (1 - smooth01(d / h.r)) - y) * (1 - smooth01((d / h.r - 0.85) / 0.15)); } return y; } function sbDeckTop(x, z, py) { // Oceana: the top of whichever path deck is under (x, z) that she is on or above (py = her feet; null: any), or a very low number. A deck is the path itself: as wide as its segment, tilted to its bank, ending square where the path ends, and missing wherever a gap cuts it var B = SB; if (!B || B.terrain !== 'level2') return -1e9; var ex = x - L3_X, top = -1e9, sm0 = B.samples, ing = false, ri, sq = -1e9, cE = 1e9, cY = -1e9; if (!sbNoRamps) for (ri = 0; ri < B.ramps.length; ri++) { // a ramp: an exact wedge standing on the deck (on Oceana it is not part of the ground either). Solid from every side: it counts whatever her height var rp = B.ramps[ri]; if (rp.base === undefined || Math.abs(rp.x - ex) > rp.rr || Math.abs(rp.z - z) > rp.rr) continue; var rdx = ex - rp.x, rdz = z - rp.z, rlf = rdx * Math.sin(rp.th) - rdz * Math.cos(rp.th), rlx = rdx * Math.cos(rp.th) + rdz * Math.sin(rp.th); if (Math.abs(rlx) <= rp.hw && rlf >= -rp.hl && rlf <= rp.hl) { var ry = rp.base + rp.tn * (rlf + rp.hl) + rp.y; if (ry > top) top = ry; } } sbNear(B.gapH, ex, z, B.maxG, function (e) { if (!ing && sbIn(e, ex, z, 0)) ing = true; }); if (ing) return top; sbNear(B.pathH, ex, z, B.hwMax + 6, function (pa) { // every stretch that starts at a sample within reach (so where two paths cross, each is found, and her height picks between them) var pb = sm0[pa.i + 1]; if (!pb || pb.brk || (pa.wl && pb.wl) || (pa.dsk && pb.dsk)) return; var vx = pb.x - pa.x, vz = pb.z - pa.z, vl = vx * vx + vz * vz; if (vl < 1e-9) return; var tr = ((ex - pa.x) * vx + (z - pa.z) * vz) / vl; if ((tr < 0 && pa.startP) || (tr > 1 && pb.endP)) return; // past the very start or end of the path: no deck, and no floor var tt = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, tr)), dq = Math.hypot(ex - pa.x - vx * tt, z - pa.z - vz * tt), hwq = ((pa.w || B.width) + ((pb.w || B.width) - (pa.w || B.width)) * tt) / 2; if (dq > hwq) return; var yy = pa.y + (pb.y - pa.y) * tt, rl = (pa.roll || 0) + ((pb.roll || 0) - (pa.roll || 0)) * tt; if (rl > 0.05 || rl < -0.05) { var lat = ((ex - pa.x) * -vz + (z - pa.z) * vx) / Math.sqrt(vl) + (pb.th - pa.th) * Math.sqrt(vl) * tt * (1 - tt) / 2; hwq *= Math.cos(rl * Math.PI / 180); if (dq > hwq) return; yy -= Math.max(-hwq, Math.min(hwq, lat)) * Math.tan(rl * Math.PI / 180); } // a banked deck: only cos(bank) as wide across the ground, the right side lower for a positive bank (and the arc's own bulge between two samples added back, as for the ground) if (py !== null && py < yy - Math.max((pb.dk || DECK_TH) + 0.25, 0.45 * Math.abs(Math.tan(rl * Math.PI / 180)) + 0.3)) return; // her feet are below its underside: she is under it (an overpass), not on it. Anywhere within its thickness it is solid: its top is her floor, so from beside it, lower down, it stands in her way like a ledge instead of letting her walk into its side, and a steep bank cannot be climbed sideways through the deck var ext = (tr < 0 ? -tr : tr > 1 ? tr - 1 : 0) * Math.sqrt(vl); // how far past this stretch's own end the point lies (0: squarely alongside it) if (ext < 1e-6) { if (yy > sq) sq = yy; } // the height comes from the stretch she is alongside. (Taking the highest of every stretch whose rounded end reached her lifted her off a climbing path by its slope times its half width) else if (ext < cE - 1e-6) { cE = ext; cY = yy; } else if (ext < cE + 1e-6 && yy > cY) cY = yy; // alongside none (the outside of a corner): the nearest stretch's end fills it }); if (sq < -1e8) sq = cY; if (sq > -1e8 && !sbNoBump && (B.dLedgeH || B.dHills.length)) sq = sbDeckHill(ex, z, sq) + sbDeckLedge(ex, z); // ledges and hills standing on the deck if (sq > top) top = sq; for (var di2 = 0; di2 < B.discs.length; di2++) { // a circular deck var Dq = B.discs[di2]; if (Math.hypot(ex - Dq.cx, z - Dq.cz) > Dq.R) continue; var dyq = sbDiscY(Dq, ex, z); if (py !== null && py < dyq - (Dq.dk || DECK_TH) - 0.25) continue; if (dyq > top) top = dyq; } return top; } function l3Top(x, z) { var y = level3H(x, z), d = sbDeckTop(x, z, null); return d > y ? d : y; } // the Sandbox ground at a point, or the path deck over it (Oceana), whichever is higher function sbSlabTop(x, z, py) { // the top of whichever floating slab of a path is under (x, z) that she is on or above (py = her feet; null: any), or a very low number var B = SB; if (!B || !B.slabH) return -1e9; var ex = x - L3_X, top = -1e9, sm0 = B.samples, cE = 1e9, cY = -1e9; sbNear(B.slabH, ex, z, B.hwMax + 5, function (bp) { for (var di = -1; di <= 0; di++) { var pa = sm0[bp.i + di], pb = sm0[bp.i + di + 1]; if (!pa || !pb || pb.brk || !pb.fl) continue; var vx = pb.x - pa.x, vz = pb.z - pa.z, vl = vx * vx + vz * vz; if (vl < 1e-9) continue; var tr = ((ex - pa.x) * vx + (z - pa.z) * vz) / vl; if ((tr < 0 && pa.startP) || (tr > 1 && pb.endP)) continue; // past the very start or end of the path: the slab ends square there, as it is drawn, and there is no floor beyond it var tt = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, tr)), qx = pa.x + vx * tt, qz = pa.z + vz * tt, dq = Math.hypot(ex - qx, z - qz); var hwq = ((pa.w || B.width) + ((pb.w || B.width) - (pa.w || B.width)) * tt) / 2, yy = pa.y + (pb.y - pa.y) * tt, rl = (pa.roll || 0) + ((pb.roll || 0) - (pa.roll || 0)) * tt; if (rl > 0.05 || rl < -0.05) { var lat = ((ex - pa.x) * -vz + (z - pa.z) * vx) / Math.sqrt(vl) + (pb.th - pa.th) * Math.sqrt(vl) * tt * (1 - tt) / 2; hwq *= Math.cos(rl * Math.PI / 180); yy -= Math.max(-hwq, Math.min(hwq, lat)) * Math.tan(rl * Math.PI / 180); } if (dq > hwq) continue; if (py !== null && py < yy - Math.max(pb.fl + 0.25, 0.45 * Math.abs(Math.tan(rl * Math.PI / 180)) + 0.3)) continue; // her feet are below its underside: she is under it. Within its thickness it is solid (it used to let her in from the side, or up through a steep bank, once she was 1.2 below its top) var ext = (tr < 0 ? -tr : tr > 1 ? tr - 1 : 0) * Math.sqrt(vl); if (ext < 1e-6) { if (yy > top) top = yy; } // the height comes from the stretch she is squarely alongside (the highest of every stretch whose rounded end reached her lifted her off a climbing slab by its slope times its half width) else if (ext < cE - 1e-6) { cE = ext; cY = yy; } else if (ext < cE + 1e-6 && yy > cY) cY = yy; // alongside none (the outside of a corner): the nearest stretch's end fills it } }); return top > -1e8 ? top : cY; } // ---- Walls: a path banked past 80 degrees is a tilted plane, not part of the height map (a height map cannot hold a vertical face). She moves on it as she would on flat ground: // her heading is measured along the wall (0 = along the path, + = toward the higher side), the camera turns the same way, and the pull is the slope table's. ---- function sbWallFrame(i, t) { var sm0 = SB.samples, pa = sm0[i], pb = sm0[i + 1], vx = pb.x - pa.x, vz = pb.z - pa.z, L = Math.hypot(vx, vz) || 1e-6, fx = vx / L, fz = vz / L, r = ((pa.roll || 0) + ((pb.roll || 0) - (pa.roll || 0)) * t) * Math.PI / 180; return { i: i, t: t, L: L, fx: fx, fz: fz, lx: -fz, lz: fx, cx: pa.x + vx * t, cz: pa.z + vz * t, yc: pa.y + (pb.y - pa.y) * t, r: r, cr: Math.cos(r), sr: Math.sin(r), hw: ((pa.w || SB.width) + ((pb.w || SB.width) - (pa.w || SB.width)) * t) / 2, th: Math.atan2(fx, -fz), p: (pb.y - pa.y) / L }; } function sbWallFind(ex, z, y) { // the wall piece nearest (ex, z, y), with where she is on it: a (across, + toward the lower side) and d (out from the face) var best = null, sm0 = SB.samples; sbNear(SB.wallH, ex, z, SB.hwMax + 8, function (p0) { var i = p0.i, pa = sm0[i], pb = sm0[i + 1]; if (!pb) return; var vx = pb.x - pa.x, vz = pb.z - pa.z, L = Math.hypot(vx, vz); if (L < 1e-6) return; var t = ((ex - pa.x) * vx + (z - pa.z) * vz) / (L * L), tc = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t)), over = Math.abs(t - tc) * L; if (over > 0.8) return; var w = sbWallFrame(i, tc), hl = (ex - w.cx) * w.lx + (z - w.cz) * w.lz, dy = y - w.yc; w.a = hl * w.cr - dy * w.sr; w.d = hl * w.sr + dy * w.cr; w.sc = Math.abs(w.d) + over * 2; if (!best || w.sc < best.sc) best = w; }); return best; } function sbWallLeave(w, vf, vl, kn, now) { // off the wall: her speed along it and the kick away from it become an ordinary flight var pr = w.p || 0, q = Math.sqrt(1 + pr * pr * w.cr * w.cr), u = vf / q; // her speed along the wall follows its rise var vx = u * (w.fx + w.sr * pr * w.lx * w.cr) - vl * w.lx * w.cr + kn * w.lx * w.sr, vz = u * (w.fz + w.sr * pr * w.lz * w.cr) - vl * w.lz * w.cr + kn * w.lz * w.sr, vyy = u * pr * w.cr * w.cr + vl * w.sr + kn * w.cr, hs = Math.hypot(vx, vz), h0 = heading; if (hs > 0.5) heading = Math.atan2(-vx, -vz); speed = hs; vy = vyy; grounded = false; wallOn = false; wallCool = now + 250; yaw += wrapA(heading - h0); fallTop = player.position.y; } var wallPY = 0, wallPN = 0, wallQX = 0, wallQY = 0, wallQZ = 0, WALL_STEEP = 60; // WALL_STEEP: she cannot run into a surface this many degrees steeper than the one she is on (it stops her like a wall) function sbWallBump(w, qx, qz, qy, now) { // too steep to run into: put her back where she was, keep only what runs along the face, and treat the hit like any other collision var pp = player.position, Hx = -Math.sin(heading), Hz = -Math.cos(heading), along = Hx * w.fx + Hz * w.fz, hn = Math.abs(Hx * w.lx + Hz * w.lz), h0 = heading, sg = along >= 0 ? 1 : -1; pp.set(qx, qy, qz); var endOn = Math.abs(along) > hn, into = speed * (endOn ? Math.abs(along) : hn); if (endOn) speed = 0; // running straight at the foot of the wall along the path: it stops her dead else { heading = Math.atan2(-sg * w.fx, -sg * w.fz); speed *= Math.abs(along); yaw += Math.max(-0.8, Math.min(0.8, wrapA(heading - h0))); } // coming at it from the side: she glances off along it if (now >= bumpUntil && into > 0.5) { bumpUntil = now + 1000; if (into >= 50 * TILE && !ballOn && hp > 0) { hp -= 1; drawHearts(); flashHurt(); } else shake(0.28); } } function sbWallEnter(now) { var pp0 = player.position, qx = wallQX, qy = wallQY, qz = wallQZ; wallQX = pp0.x; wallQY = pp0.y; wallQZ = pp0.z; var rise = (now > wallPN && now - wallPN < 200) ? (pp0.y - wallPY) / ((now - wallPN) / 1000) : 0; wallPY = pp0.y; wallPN = now; // how fast she was rising or falling a moment ago if (!SB || !SB.wallH || level !== 3 || wallOn || !playing || now < wallCool) return; var pp = player.position, w = sbWallFind(pp.x - L3_X, pp.z, pp.y); if (grounded && w && Math.abs(w.a) <= w.hw + 0.5 && Math.abs(w.r) * 57.29578 - slDeg > WALL_STEEP) { // a much steeper face than the ground she is on: she hits it instead of running up it var hlP = (pp.x - L3_X - w.cx) * w.lx + (pp.z - w.cz) * w.lz, hlQ = (qx - L3_X - w.cx) * w.lx + (qz - w.cz) * w.lz; if (Math.abs(w.d) < 1.5 || hlP * hlQ < 0) { sbWallBump(w, qx, qz, qy, now); return; } } if (!w || Math.abs(w.a) > w.hw || w.d < -0.8 || w.d > (grounded ? 1.0 : 0.7)) return; var Hx = -Math.sin(heading), Hz = -Math.cos(heading), vf, vl; var vyE = grounded ? Math.max(-speed, Math.min(speed, rise)) : vy, hsE = grounded ? Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, speed * speed - vyE * vyE)) : speed; // her real velocity: the run on the ground, and however fast she was climbing the bank var vx3 = Hx * hsE, vz3 = Hz * hsE, vh = vx3 * w.lx + vz3 * w.lz, pr = w.p, q = Math.sqrt(1 + pr * pr * w.cr * w.cr); vf = (vx3 * w.fx + vz3 * w.fz + vyE * pr + w.sr * pr * (vh * w.cr - vyE * w.sr)) / q; vl = -vh * w.cr + vyE * w.sr; // her 3D velocity on the wall's surface (along it, following its rise, and across its face): the part into the wall is lost, so hitting a wall head-on does not turn her speed straight up it if (vh * w.sr + vyE * w.cr > 2) return; // moving away from its face (the wall's own rise or fall along its length does not count: she sticks to it as she does to ground that tips gently away) var h0 = heading; speed = Math.hypot(vf, vl); heading = wrapA(-w.th + Math.atan2(vl, vf)); yaw += wrapA(heading - h0); wallOn = true; grounded = true; vy = 0; wallR = w.r; wallNx = w.lx * w.sr; wallNz = w.lz * w.sr; sgx = sgz = 0; var a = Math.max(-w.hw, Math.min(w.hw, w.a)); pp.set(L3_X + w.cx + w.lx * a * w.cr, w.yc - a * w.sr, w.cz + w.lz * a * w.cr); } function sbWallMove(sdt, now) { var pp = player.position, w = sbWallFind(pp.x - L3_X, pp.z, pp.y); if (!w) { wallOn = false; grounded = false; wallCool = now + 250; return; } var ps = wrapA(heading + w.th), vf = speed * Math.cos(ps), vl = speed * Math.sin(ps); var pr = w.p, q = Math.sqrt(1 + pr * pr * w.cr * w.cr); // the wall's rise per unit along its plan, and how much longer its surface is than its plan vf -= GRAV * pr * w.cr * w.cr / q * sdt; // gravity (straight down) on the wall's surface: the part along the wall (a climb costs speed, a descent adds it) vl -= GRAV * w.sr * sdt; // and the part across its face, toward the lower side speed = Math.hypot(vf, vl); if (speed > 0.5) heading += wrapA(Math.atan2(vl, vf) - ps); // the pull really bends her direction (a true vector sum that is kept from frame to frame), so only her grip can hold her line if (jumpBuf > 0) { jumpBuf = 0; var kn = (spaceDown ? JUMP_V : JUMP_V * 0.35) * 0.7; sbWallLeave(w, vf, vl, kn, now); return; } var ds = vf * sdt / q, sm0 = SB.samples, i = w.i, L = w.L, t = w.t + ds / L, end = 0, a2 = w.a - vl * sdt + w.sr * pr * ds; // moved along the surface in 3D while (t > 1) { var nx = sm0[i + 2]; if (nx && sm0[i + 1].wl && nx.wl && !nx.brk) { var L2 = Math.hypot(nx.x - sm0[i + 1].x, nx.z - sm0[i + 1].z) || 1e-6; t = (t - 1) * L / L2; L = L2; i++; } else { t = 1; end = 1; break; } } while (t < 0) { var pv = sm0[i - 1]; if (pv && pv.wl && sm0[i].wl && !sm0[i].brk) { var L3 = Math.hypot(sm0[i].x - pv.x, sm0[i].z - pv.z) || 1e-6; t = 1 + t * L / L3; L = L3; i--; } else { t = 0; end = -1; break; } } var wn = sbWallFrame(i, t), dH = wrapA(w.th - wn.th); // the wall bends: her heading and the camera turn with it, so her angle along the wall is kept heading += dH; yaw += dH; wallR = wn.r; wallNx = wn.lx * wn.sr; wallNz = wn.lz * wn.sr; var side = Math.abs(a2) > wn.hw, ac = Math.max(-wn.hw, Math.min(wn.hw, a2)); pp.set(L3_X + wn.cx + wn.lx * ac * wn.cr, wn.yc - ac * wn.sr, wn.cz + wn.lz * ac * wn.cr); if (side || end) { var ps2 = wrapA(heading + wn.th), vf2 = speed * Math.cos(ps2), vl2 = speed * Math.sin(ps2), sp0 = speed; sbWallLeave(wn, vf2, vl2, 0, now); if (end && !side) { var gH = groundH(pp.x, pp.z); if (Math.abs(pp.y - gH) < 0.8) { grounded = true; vy = 0; speed = sp0; pp.y = gH; } } // the wall runs out onto the ground: she carries on along it } } function sbWallMeshes() { var B = SB; if (!B.wallH) return; var sm = B.samples, P = [], C = [], col = new THREE.Color(), mat = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ vertexColors: true, flatShading: true, side: THREE.DoubleSide }), i, tc = 0, TH = 1.5; function quad(a, b, c, d, h, sat, l) { var j = rand01(tc++, 5, 73); col.setHSL(h + (j - 0.5) * 0.01, sat, l + (j - 0.5) * 0.03); [a, b, c, a, c, d].forEach(function (v) { P.push(v[0], v[1], v[2]); C.push(col.r, col.g, col.b); }); } function E(sp) { var hw = (sp.w || B.width) / 2, r = (sp.roll || 0) * Math.PI / 180, cr = Math.cos(r), sr = Math.sin(r), lx = Math.cos(sp.th), lz = Math.sin(sp.th), x = L3_X + sp.x, z = sp.z; var nx = lx * sr * TH, ny = cr * TH, nz = lz * sr * TH; } function sbSlabCut(ex, z, y) { // the height the ground is brought down to under and beside a floating slab (just below its underside, easing back out over 6 units), or y if it is already lower var B = SB, sm0 = B.samples, out = y; sbNear(B.slabH, ex, z, B.hwMax + 12, function (bp) { for (var di = -1; di <= 0; di++) { var pa = sm0[bp.i + di], pb = sm0[bp.i + di + 1]; if (!pa || !pb || pb.brk || !pb.fl) continue; var vx = pb.x - pa.x, vz = pb.z - pa.z, vl = vx * vx + vz * vz; // and the same past its end var tt = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, tr)), dq = Math.hypot(ex - (pa.x + vx * tt), z - (pa.z + vz * tt)); var hwq = ((pa.w || B.width) + ((pb.w || B.width) - (pa.w || B.width)) * tt) / 2 + 1, k = 1 - smooth01((dq - hwq) / 6); if (k <= 0) continue; var bot = pa.y + (pb.y - pa.y) * tt - pb.fl - 0.5, rl = Math.abs((pa.roll || 0) + ((pb.roll || 0) - (pa.roll || 0)) * tt); if (rl > 0.05) bot -= (hwq - 1) * Math.sin(Math.min(rl, 80) * Math.PI / 180); // a banked slab's low edge if (y > bot) { var c = y + (bot - y) * k; if (c < out) out = c; } } }); return out; } function sbSlabMeshes() { // each floating stretch of path: a top, an underside, two sides and a cap at each end, as one slab of stone var B = SB; if (!B.slabH) return; var sm = B.samples, P = [], C = [], col = new THREE.Color(), mat = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ vertexColors: true, flatShading: true, side: THREE.DoubleSide }), i, tc = 0; function quad(a, b, c, d, h, sat, l) { var j = rand01(tc++, 5, 71); } function boostZoneMesh(strips, hf) { // the last stretch of a ramp, where a jump gives a Style Boost: a red and white checkerboard laid just above the ground. strips: [x0, z0, forwardX, forwardZ, rightX, rightZ, from, to, halfWidth] along and across the ramp, heights from the ground there var P = [], C = [], CELL = 2.5; strips.forEach(function (q) { var nl = Math.max(1, Math.round((q[7] - q[6]) / CELL)), nw = Math.max(1, Math.round(q[8] * 2 / CELL)), dl = (q[7] - q[6]) / nl, dw = q[8] * 2 / nw, i, k; function pt(a, b) { var x = q[0] + q[2] * a + q[4] * b, z = q[1] + q[3] * a + q[5] * b; [0.95, 0.95, 0.95] : [0.86, 0.1, 0.1]; [p00, p10, p11, p00, p11, p01].forEach(function (v) { P.push(v[0], v[1], v[2]); C.push(c[0], c[1], c[2]); }); } }); if (!P.length) return null; var g = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); m.renderOrder = 2; scene.add(m); return m; } var l2BoostM = null; function l2BoostZone() { // Oceana Crags' ramps: the last L2_STYLE_ZONE of each if (l2BoostM || L2_BARE) return; var st = []; L2_PIECES.forEach(function (pc) { if (pc.ramp) st.push([pc.x0, pc.z0, pc.hx, pc.hz, -pc.hz, pc.hx, pc.len - L2_STYLE_ZONE, pc.len, L2_HW]); }); l2BoostM = boostZoneMesh(st); } function sbRampInit() { // each ramp's foot: the height she would stand on there without it (on Oceana's slab of path that is the path, not the water far below) if (!SB) return; }); } finally { sbNoRamps = false; } sbRampMeshes(); } function sbRampMeshes() { // Oceana: each ramp drawn as the exact wedge she stands on (elsewhere a ramp is part of the ground and its mesh) var B = SB; if (!B || B.terrain !== 'level2' || !B.ramps.length) return; var P = [], C = [], col = new THREE.Color(), tc = 0; function tri(a, b, c, k) { var j1 = rand01(tc, 17, 93), j2 = rand01(tc, 5, 94); tc++; col.setHSL(0.085 + (j1 - 0.5) * 0.012, 0.19 + (j1 - 0.5) * 0.05, (0.50 + (j1 - 0.5) * 0.07 + (j2 - 0.5) * 0.03) * k); [a, b, c].forEach(function (v) { P.push(v[0], v[1], v[2]); C.push(col.r, col.g, col.b); }); } B.ramps.forEach(function (rp) { if (rp.base === undefined) return; var fx = Math.sin(rp.th), fz = -Math.cos(rp.th), lx = Math.cos(rp.th), lz = Math.sin(rp.th), y0 = rp.base + rp.y, yb = Math.min(y0, rp.base), nl = Math.max(1, Math.round(rp.hl * 2 / 2.5)), nw = Math.max(1, Math.round(rp.hw * 2 / 2.5)), dl = rp.hl * 2 / nl, dw = rp.hw * 2 / nw, i, k; function pt(a, b, top) { return [L3_X + rp.x + fx * a + lx * b, top ? y0 + rp.tn * (a + rp.hl) : yb, rp.z + fz * a + lz * b]; } // a along it (from -half length at its foot), b across it for (i = 0; i < nl; }); if (bz.length) { var m = boostZoneMesh(bz, sbFoot); if (m) SB.meshes.push(m); } } function pathTexCol(col, tx, s, u, j1, j2, k) { // a path surface's colour for one facet: 0 the light brown stone, 1 wooden planks across the path, 2 grey metal plates in a checker, 3 grass. s, u: where the facet is along and across the path; j1, j2: its own small random shade k = k || 1; if (tx === 1) { var pl = Math.floor(s / 2.5), pr = rand01(pl, 3, 77); col.setHSL(0.072 + (j1 - 0.5) * 0.01 + (pr - 0.5) * 0.008, 0.46, ((pl & 1 ? 0.34 : 0.28) + (pr - 0.5) * 0.04 + (j1 - 0.5) * 0.025) * k); } else if (tx === 2) { var ck = (Math.floor(s / 5) + Math.floor((u + 40) / 5)) & 1; col.setHSL(0.6, 0.07, ((ck ? 0.46 : 0.38) + (j1 - 0.5) * 0.025) * k); } else if (tx === 3) { col.setHSL(0.265 + (j1 - 0.5) * 0.02, 0.5, (0.32 + (j1 - 0.5) * 0.05 + (j2 - 0.5) * 0.02) * k); } else col.setHSL(0.085 + (j1 - 0.5) * 0.012, 0.19 + (j1 - 0.5) * 0.05, (0.50 + (j1 - 0.5) * 0.07 + (j2 - 0.5) * 0.03) * k); } var sbPyOv; // when set (a height, or null for any), the height to treat as the feet when finding a slab or platform top: objects and mobs use it so they stand on a slab instead of the ground under it function sbFoot(wx, wz) { sbPyOv = null; var h = groundH(wx, wz); sbPyOv = undefined; return h; } function sbMovPy(x, z) { if (sbPyOv !== undefined) return sbPyOv; var pp = player.position; return (x - pp.x < 8 && pp.x - x < 8 && z - pp.z < 8 && pp.z - z < 8) ? pp.y : null; } function sbMoverInit() { SB.movers.forEach(function (m) { m.base = l3Top(L3_X + m.pts[0][0], m.pts[0][1]); if (m.o && m.o.T !== undefined) m.base = m.door ? m.o.T : m.o.T - m.hgt; }); } // a true Y: a platform's top (at its start), a door's foot function sbRampEnd(x, z) { // on the last few units of a ramp: a jump from here is a Style Boost if (!SB) return false; }); } function sbCross(B, g, ex, ez, py) { // did she cross the plane of g (facing its heading) between last frame and this one? Where, sideways and up? var fx = Math.sin(g.th), fz = -Math.cos(g.th), rx = Math.cos(g.th), rz = Math.sin(g.th); var f0 = (B.px - g.x) * fx + (B.pz - g.z) * fz, f1 = (ex - g.x) * fx + (ez - g.z) * fz; if (f0 * f1 > 0 || f0 === f1 || Math.abs(f0 - f1) > 80) return null; var tt = f0 / (f0 - f1); return { lat: ((B.px - g.x) + (ex - B.px) * tt) * rx + ((B.pz - g.z) + (ez - B.pz) * tt) * rz, yy: B.py + (py - B.py) * tt }; } // ---- Pressure plates and targets: interactables a door can be linked to (a door counts a plate as beaten while it is pressed, a target once it is broken) ---- var sbPlateTex = {}; function sbPlateTexture(kind) { // a tiling texture, 8 units across: pale stone for gray, sanded planks for brown if (sbPlateTex[kind]) return sbPlateTex[kind]; var cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = cv.height = 128; var g = cv.getContext('2d'), i, rr = function (k) { var x = Math.sin(k * 127.1 + 311.7) * 43758.5453; return x - Math.floor(x); }; if (kind === 'brown') { g.fillStyle = '#c4a07a'; g.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128); for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) { g.fillStyle = i % 2 ? 'rgba(120,80,45,.10)' : 'rgba(255,240,210,.10)'; g.fillRect(0, i * 32, 128, 32); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(95,62,35,.45)'; g.fillRect(0, i * 32, 128, 1.5); } g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(110,72,40,.22)'; g.lineWidth = 1; for (i = 0; i < 14; i++) { var yy = rr(i) * 128; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, yy); g.bezierCurveTo(40, yy - 3, 90, yy + 3, 128, yy); g.stroke(); } } else { g.fillStyle = '#c3c4c0'; g.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128); return t; } function sbPlateY(pl, wx, wz) { return pl.T !== undefined ? pl.T : sbFoot(wx, wz) + pl.y; } function sbTrigMeshes() { var B = SB; if (!B) return; B.plates.forEach(function (pl) { if (pl.look === 'none') return; var cs = Math.cos(pl.th), sn = Math.sin(pl.th), loc = function (lx, lz) { return [pl.x + lx * cs - lz * sn, pl.z + lx * sn + lz * cs]; }, grid = [], uv = [], pos = [], uvs = [], nu, nv, iu, iv, rim = [];
